We characterize how CI services are adopted, evolved, and abandoned over time through quantitative and qualitative analyses. About one in five repositories use multiple CI services, often transitional during migration rather than for sustained parallel use. CI service choice is the dominant factor across adoption, complexity, maintenance, and engagement, with language effects limited to specific cases. Our results reveal that the shift to GitHub Actions was a synchronized platform-level transition, crossing 50\% of first adoptions in 2020 across all studied languages, while the decline of Travis CI following its pricing change became the strongest abandonment signal. Our analysis of CI configuration rationales shows that activity is mostly reactive: only about 11\% of commits state a strategic reason, indicating that commit messages capture operational maintenance patterns more reliably than strategic intent. Finally, we find that multi-CI adoption is primarily associated with project maturity, whereas abandonment is more strongly associated with service identity than repository characteristics. These findings provide empirical evidence for CI tooling, migration support, and future research on managing evolving software delivery infrastructure.

</details>


### [313] [WatchPoint: Executable User Feedback for Real-World Agentic Web Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26204)
*Guanqun Yang,Wei Yang,Xueqing Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出 WatchPoint,一种模拟用户系统,通过生成并执行诊断脚本对运行中的 Web 应用进行交互,产生结构化观察结果以指导编码模型重试,在 Web-Bench 上恢复了 57.6% 的失败任务,与人类测试者的 54.5% 相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码代理的反馈机制依赖截图、LLM 打分或自然语言纠正,很少像真实开发者那样与运行中的实时应用交互,因此难以模拟开发者通过点击、检查样式、运行诊断命令来定位问题的行为。

Method: 提出 WatchPoint 模拟用户系统,模仿真实开发者行为,针对运行中的应用生成并执行诊断脚本,输出结构化观察结果来引导编码模型的重新尝试。工作基于 Web-Bench,包含 50 个多文件 Web 项目、1000 个顺序依赖任务,由确定性端到端测试验证。

Result: WatchPoint 在其诊断的任务中恢复了 57.6%;受控用户研究显示人类测试者恢复率为 54.5%,验证了模拟的真实性。研究还识别出一类能力差距模式,决定模拟用户反馈何时有效、何时应当保留。

Conclusion: 自动化诊断脚本可在顺序 Web 开发任务上替代交互式人工测试,并为何时使用模拟用户反馈提供了依据。

Abstract: When a professional web developer's code fails a test, they do not simply re-read the stack trace. They open the application in a browser, click buttons, inspect computed styles, and run diagnostic commands to understand what went wrong. Existing feedback mechanisms for coding agents rely on screenshots, LLM-as-a-judge scoring, or natural-language corrections, but few interact with the live application the way a developer would. We introduce WatchPoint, a simulated-user system that mimics real developer behavior by generating and executing diagnostic scripts against the running application, producing structured observations that guide the coding model's retry. Unlike prior approaches that target single-file edits or evaluate using non-executable metrics, we operate on Web-Bench, a benchmark of 50 multi-file web projects comprising 1,000 sequentially dependent tasks, verified by deterministic end-to-end tests. WatchPoint recovers 57.6% of the tasks it diagnoses, and a controlled user study confirms the simulation's realism: human testers achieve a comparable recovery rate (54.5%), providing evidence that automated diagnostic scripts can substitute for interactive human testing on sequential web development tasks. We further identify a pattern of capability gaps that governs when simulated-user feedback is helpful and when it should be withheld.

</details>


### [314] [Reducing Hallucinations in Large Language Models Through Integrated Self-Verification and Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26229)
*Ashly Joseph*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出CoVe-RAG+框架，将链式验证（CoVe）与检索增强生成（RAG）结合，以减少LLM在工程任务中的幻觉，事实准确率较基线提升28%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM已逐渐用于CAD文档、标准合规验证、知识检索等高级工程任务，但易产生看似合理却缺乏依据的幻觉，影响其在追求精度与合规的高端工程场景中的可信度。

Method: CoVe-RAG+统一框架，将Chain-of-Verification与RAG集成；支持LLM在权威外部来源（工程标准、CAD信息、仿真报告）中进行验证，并采用迭代式自验证流程核实关键论断。

Result: 在CAD模型文档、标准合规验证、历史设计数据复用等工程任务上评估，事实准确率较CoVe与RAG基线方法提升28%；并通过可解释的验证报告与来源可追溯性增强用户信任。

Conclusion: CoVe-RAG+为在事实准确性至关重要的工程设计流程中部署LLM提供了可扩展且可靠的方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are progressively used for advanced engineering tasks, includes Computer-Aided Design (CAD) documentation, standards compliance verification, and knowledge retrieval. Still, they are prone to produce hallucinations, outputs that seem convincing but aren't based on context that limit their trustworthiness in high-end engineering applications where precision and compliance are crucial. The paper introduces CoVe-RAG+, a unified framework that integrates Chain-of-Verification (CoVe) with Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) to mitigate hallucinations in the results generated by large language models (LLMs). CoVe-RAG+ supports LLM verification in external sources of authority, such as engineering standards, CAD information, and simulation reports, while applying an iterative self-verification process to validate important claims. CoVe-RAG+ is assessed on engineering activities such as CAD model documentation, standards compliance verification, and the reutilization of historical design data. Experimental findings indicate a 28% improvement in factual accuracy relative to baseline CoVe and RAG methodologies. Moreover, CoVe-RAG+ strengthens user confidence by providing elucidative verification reports and source traceability. The findings indicate that CoVe-RAG+ provides a scalable and reliable option for implementing LLMs in engineering design processes where factual accuracy is critical.

</details>


### [315] [CANcept: Model-based CAN Traffic Generation and Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26263)
*Lino Wertz,Junes Sheikhi,Florian Fehrle,Adrian Rupp,Tianhai Liu,Philipp Kern,Carsten Sinz,Bernhard Beckert*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出开源模型驱动工具，用于CAN流量的建模、生成、回放与操控，便于测试基于CAN软件的时间安全属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试CAN软件的时间相关安全属性需要精确控制报文内容与通信时序，而现有开源工具用低级脚本编码场景，难以维护和演进。

Method: 工具采用两个模型：流量调度模型（TSM）定义报文时序与内容变换，基于DBC的通信模型（DCM）定义报文集流量与CAN帧之间的编解码；两者在同一执行机制中结合，支持生成与操控流量，并可回放变换后的轨迹。

Result: 初步评估表明，该工具能实现设定场景，时序偏差低，在高流量速率下执行稳定。

Conclusion: 基于模型的工具为CAN时间相关安全属性测试提供了易维护、可演进的方案，并展现出良好的时序精度与稳定性。

Abstract: Testing timing-related safety properties of Controller Area Network (CAN)-based software requires precise control over transmitted data and communication timing. Existing open-source tools typically encode such scenarios in low-level scripts, making them difficult to maintain and evolve. We present \toolName{}, an open-source model-based tool for specifying, generating, replaying, and manipulating CAN traffic. A traffic schedule model (TSM) defines message timing and content transformations, and a DBC-based communication model (DCM) defines encoding and decoding between message-level traffic and CAN frames. \toolName{} combines both models in one execution mechanism for generated and manipulated traffic, including replay of transformed traces. A preliminary evaluation indicates that \toolName{} realizes the specified scenarios, with low timing deviation and stable execution under elevated traffic rates.

</details>


### [316] [On the Lexical Superstition of Large Language Models for Code Comprehension: Re-evaluation on Code of Low Lexical Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26388)
*Xin Shen,San-Zhuo Xi,Yali Du,Ming Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Face/Off语义保持的标识符重命名框架，发现当前LLM在代码理解任务中普遍过度依赖词法线索（标识符名称），忽视程序结构语义，且该问题在多种干预下依然顽固。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标识符名称在自然代码中具有统计信息价值，但其信息并不总是可靠的。作者想探究当前LLM在程序结构保持不变的情况下，是否会赋予词法线索（即标识符名称）不成比例的权重。

Method: 引入Face/Off——一个语义保持的标识符重命名框架，在多个模型和代码理解任务上评估渐进式命名条件（逐步移除或误导标识符信息）；并测试了基于提示和微调的干预方法，以及一个类型推断的对照实验来检验边界条件。

Result: 词法过度依赖在所有评估模型和主要任务中普遍存在：随着标识符信息被移除或变得误导，性能普遍下降，且输出常被引向误导性名称所暗示的语义。该模式在提示与微调干预下依然持续。类型推断对照确认了边界：当答案可在无目标名称情况下局部恢复时，命名效应较小。

Conclusion: 结果并非表明标识符无用，而是揭示了当前LLM在平衡词法线索与程序结构方面存在系统性脆弱性。研究呼吁开发新的评估与建模方法，在保留自然代码规律益处的同时，使结论建立在准确、形式化的代码语义之上。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have made them widely used for code-related tasks. Identifier names are statistically informative in naturally occurring code, but their information is not always reliable. We investigate whether current LLMs assign disproportionate weight to lexical cues when renaming preserves program structure. We introduce Face/Off, a semantics-preserving identifier-renaming framework, and evaluate progressive naming conditions across multiple models and code-comprehension tasks. Within this framework, lexical overemphasis is pervasive across the evaluated models and primary tasks: performance generally decreases as identifier information is removed or made misleading, and outputs are often directed toward the meanings suggested by misleading names. The pattern persists under representative prompt- and fine-tuning-based interventions, suggesting that lexical overemphasis is an entrenched problem. A type-inference control confirms a boundary: naming effects are smaller when the answer is locally recoverable without the target name. These results do not imply that identifiers are unhelpful; rather, they reveal a systematic vulnerability in how current LLMs balance lexical cues against program structure. Our findings motivate evaluations and modeling methods that preserve the benefits of natural code regularities while keeping conclusions grounded in accurate, formalized code semantics.

</details>


### [317] [FeatLens: Feature-Guided Dynamic Code Graph Construction and Retrieval for Repository-Level Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26480)
*Xutian Li,Bo Xiong,Yifeng Zhu,Kunze Li,Xianlin Zhao,Runbang Yan,Yanzhen Zou,Lu Zhang,Bing Xie*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: FeatLens 提出一种特征引导的动态代码图构建与检索方法，用轻量确定性的依赖检索替代全仓库图维护与LLM图探索，提升仓库级代码生成效果并大幅降低成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仓库级代码生成需要LLM识别可复用的仓库依赖（如已有函数、API、跨文件定义）。现有检索方法（代码相似度搜索、持久化全仓库图、LLM驱动图探索）存在图构建、推理和token开销高的问题；而面向特征的方法主要支持需求分解、规划或特征编辑，并不擅长代码依赖检索。

Method: FeatLens 构建特征索引，将自然语言特征描述与函数级代码实体关联；针对具体生成任务，从特征索引动态构建任务特定的种子图，并通过语义-结构图推理结合个性化PageRank选出紧凑的推理图。

Result: 在DevEval和EvoCodeBench上，FeatLens在稀疏、稠密及图基基线中取得最佳DR@15（0.501和0.460）。在DevEval生成任务上取得最高DIR@1，DeepSeek-V3.2为52.91%，GPT-5-mini为53.58%，同时保持竞争力的Pass@1并生成更短代码。相比最强图基基线，图节点减少61.0%、边减少86.2%、总token开销减少45.9%，检索过程不使用LLM token。

Conclusion: FeatLens 通过特征引导的动态图构建与个性化PageRank推理，实现了高效、确定且轻量的仓库级代码依赖检索，在检索与生成指标上均优于现有基线，并显著降低图规模与token成本。

Abstract: Recent code generation research has moved from isolated function completion toward repository-level generation in existing codebases. To implement a target function correctly, an LLM must identify reusable repository dependencies such as existing functions, APIs, and cross-file definitions. Existing retrieval methods provide such context through code similarity search, persistent whole-repository graphs, or LLM-driven graph exploration, but often incur high graph construction, reasoning, and token costs. Feature-oriented methods offer a natural view of software functionality, yet they mainly support requirement decomposition, planning, or feature editing rather than code dependency retrieval. This paper presents \textbf{FeatLens}, a feature-guided dynamic code graph construction and retrieval approach for repository-level code generation. FeatLens builds a feature index that links natural-language feature descriptions to function-level code entities. Given a generation task, it dynamically constructs a task-specific seed graph from the feature index and applies semantic-structural graph reasoning with personalized PageRank to select a compact reasoning graph. This design replaces persistent whole-repository graph maintenance and LLM exploration with deterministic and lightweight dependency retrieval. Experiments on DevEval and EvoCodeBench show that FeatLens achieves the best DR@15 among sparse, dense, and graph-based baselines (0.501 and 0.460). On DevEval generation, it obtains the highest DIR@1, reaching 52.91\% with DeepSeek-V3.2 and 53.58\% with GPT-5-mini, while maintaining competitive Pass@1 and producing shorter code. Compared with the strongest graph-based baseline, FeatLens reduces graph nodes by 61.0\%, edges by 86.2\%, and total token overhead by 45.9\%, with no LLM tokens used during retrieval.

</details>


### [318] [From Approval to Execution: Reconstruction-Aware Repair Analysis for LLM-Agent Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26529)
*Junchi Zhu,Zhenguang Liu,Shaojing Fan,Jianhai Chen,Qinming He*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"重构稳定授权"（ReSA）概念及 APAS-Finder 分析工具，论证修复充分性取决于最终被消费的重构动作版本，而非检查覆盖或批准记录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-agent 软件中，批准机制展示的动作常与执行时实际消费的对象不一致：工作流重载、转录投影、参数重绑定和持久状态查询都可能在执行前重构该对象。现有的字段流和检查覆盖分析只能证明预期字段被检查过，却无法保证被检查的对象版本真正到达执行汇点，也无法证明检查与使用之间没有替换介入。因此，即使局部看似完整的修复，在重构之后仍可能残留授权绕过。

Method: 三项设计：(1) 在表示转换、汇点依赖、被消费版本和授权范围之上形式化重构稳定授权（ReSA）；(2) 推导出用于评判候选修复并暴露残余汇点后缀的义务条件；(3) 在 APAS-Finder 中实现该分析，并在独立汇点预言机观察执行之前冻结预测。

Result: 预测与全部 28 个受控结果一致。四对匹配案例尽管字段流与检查覆盖完全相同，却要求相反的判定；当提供对象流、支配关系、干扰和同一授权契约时，一个 CodeQL 组合方案可恢复全部八项。两名分析师在全部四项模型验证处置上一致，在 19/20 个汇点依赖上一致。在已发布消费者上，五个修复阻止了 60/60 测试的越界效应，而三个机制对照则暴露出预测的残余效应。

Conclusion: 修复的充分性取决于被消费的重构动作，而不仅仅取决于批准记录或更早被检查的表示。

Abstract: Approval mechanisms have become a primary safeguard for consequential actions in LLM-agent software. Yet the action shown for approval is often not the object ultimately consumed: workflow reload, transcript projection, argument rebinding, and durable-state lookup may reconstruct it before execution. Existing fieldflow and check-coverage analyses can establish that expected fields were inspected, but not that the inspected object version reaches the sink or that no replacement intervenes between check and use. Consequently, a locally complete repair may still leave a residual authorization bypass after reconstruction. We address this problem through three designs. (1) We formulate reconstruction-stable authorization (ReSA) over representation transitions, sink dependencies, consumed versions, and grant scope. (2) We derive obligations that judge candidate repairs and expose residual sink suffixes. (3) We implement the analysis in APAS-Finder and freeze predictions before an independent sink oracle observes execution. Predictions agree with all 28 controlled outcomes. Four matched pairs require opposite judgments despite identical field-flow and check coverage; a CodeQL composite recovers all eight when supplied object flow, dominance, interference, and the same grant contract. Two analysts agree on all four model-validation dispositions and 19/20 sink dependencies. On released consumers, five repairs prevent 60/60 tested out-of-scope effects, while three mechanism contrasts expose the predicted residual effects. Repair sufficiency therefore depends on the reconstructed action consumed, not merely on an approval record or earlier checked representation.

</details>


### [319] [TraceVIC: Causal Reasoning over Code Evolution for Identifying Vulnerability-Inducing Commits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26711)
*Fnu Tanish,Samiha Shimmi,Samikshya Chapagain,Hamed Okhravi,Mona Rahimi,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: TraceVIC 提出基于时序图的方法，通过对代码演化进行推理来识别和排序漏洞引入提交（VIC），在 C/C++ 项目上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VIC 识别技术主要依赖 git blame 追踪易受攻击代码，并使用位置启发式（如选择最早或最近的修改），但真正的 VIC 可能出现在历史中的任意位置，且漏洞行为可能依赖跨多个修订版演化的代码。因此，VIC 识别需要推理漏洞相关代码如何演化，而非仅关注候选提交在修订历史中的位置。

Method: TraceVIC 首先定位可能的根因行，并跨修订版追踪其历史，构建图表示以捕获每个修订版内的程序结构以及漏洞相关代码在历史中的演化。随后，它利用时序边保留连续修订版之间程序元素的对应关系，对修订历史进行推理，并直接根据候选提交对漏洞条件的贡献进行排序。

Result: 消融实验表明，建模完整修订历史可将 F2 从 0.637 提升至 0.814。与最先进方法相比，TraceVIC 的 F2 最高提升 28.7%，并在四个未见过的 C/C++ 项目中为 79 个漏洞中的 78 个识别出有效的 VIC。

Conclusion: TraceVIC 通过时序图建模代码演化，有效提升 VIC 识别的准确性和排序质量，优于现有基于位置启发式的方法。

Abstract: Software vulnerabilities are often discovered long after they are introduced, making it difficult to identify the vulnerability-inducing commit (VIC) responsible for introducing the underlying vulnerable condition. Existing VIC identification techniques largely rely on git blame to trace vulnerable code through revision history and use positional heuristics, such as selecting its earliest or most recent modification. However, the true VIC may occur anywhere within this history, and vulnerable behavior may depend on code that evolves across multiple revisions. We therefore argue that VIC identification requires reasoning about how vulnerability-relevant code evolves, rather than simply where a candidate commit appears in the revision history.
  We present TraceVIC, a temporal graph-based approach for identifying and ranking VICs by reasoning over code evolution. TraceVIC first localizes likely root-cause lines and traces their histories across revisions, constructing graph representations that capture program structure within each revision and the evolution of vulnerability-relevant code across the history. It reasons over the resulting revision history, using temporal edges to preserve correspondences between program elements across consecutive revisions, and directly ranks candidate commits according to their contribution to the vulnerable condition.
  Ablation results show that modeling the full revision history improves F2 from 0.637 to 0.814. TraceVIC improves F2 by up to 28.7% over state-of-the-art methods and identifies a valid VIC for 78 of 79 vulnerabilities across four unseen C/C++ projects.

</details>


### [320] [Metrics Failure in LLM-Based Code Vulnerability Repair: An Empirical Study and a Change-Aware Screen](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26749)
*Om Nepal,Sushant Aryal,Oluseyi Olukola,Nick Rahimi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过5组对照实验（203个Big-Vul漏洞函数、3个代码LLM、3种提示策略）论证：编译率是单函数漏洞修复不可靠的评测指标，并提出change-aware的diff_F1作为低成本初筛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编译率（compile rate）被广泛用作LLM自动修复C/C++安全漏洞的进展代理指标，但作者认为它科学上不可靠，需要系统性证据揭示其缺陷，并寻找更合理的评测方向。

Method: 在Big-Vul的203个漏洞函数上，使用3个开源代码LLM（350M–6.7B）和3种提示策略开展5组对照实验，考察编译率对有效干预的敏感性、失败归因、编译器标准标志影响、与参考相似度指标的排序一致性、作为优化目标时的行为；并分析whole-function CodeBLEU与提出change-aware的diff_F1指标。

Result: 编译率：(i)对显著改善生成代码的干预几乎无响应；(ii)受评测框架与数据集伪影主导，约64%编译失败非模型所致且该比例跨模型近乎不变；(iii)单一编译器标准标志下同一补丁可变动1.8–2.7倍且无回归；(iv)对三模型的排序与参考相似度指标相反；(v)作为优化目标会奖励非修复，编译器反馈循环提高编译率但相似度下降，人工检查发现删除式与占位符式伪修复。whole-function CodeBLEU亦失效：未修改的漏洞输入得分高于所有模型。diff_F1对no-op给零分，对部分删除式作弊补丁近乎零分，但仍给真实部分编辑记分。

Conclusion: 编译率不能作为LLM漏洞修复质量的指标，whole-function CodeBLEU同样失效。diff_F1可作为深入执行级分析前的廉价初筛，但并非修复质量指标，且存在局限。研究主张采用change-aware、基于执行的评测。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly applied to the automated repair of C/C++ security vulnerabilities, and compile rate is a commonly reported proxy for progress: whether the generated patch compiles. We argue that compile rate is a scientifically unreliable metric for single-function vulnerability repair, and we support this with five controlled experiments over 203 vulnerable functions from Big-Vul, three open-source code LLMs (350M to 6.7B parameters), and three prompting strategies. Compile rate (i) barely responds to an intervention that substantially improves the generated code; (ii) is dominated by evaluation-harness and dataset artifacts rather than model quality, with about 64% of compile failures not attributable to the model, a share that is nearly invariant across models; (iii) shifts by 1.8 to 2.7 times on identical patches under a single compiler-standard flag, with zero regressions; (iv) ranks the three models in the opposite order to reference-similarity metrics; and (v) rewards non-repairs when used as an optimization target, since a compiler-feedback loop raises compile rate while similarity to the human fix falls, with manual inspection finding deletion- and placeholder-style non-repairs among the newly compiling outputs. The natural fallback, whole-function CodeBLEU, also fails: an unchanged copy of the vulnerable input outscores every model. We also examine diff_F1, a change-aware screen that scores only the edited region. It gives exactly zero credit to a no-op and near-zero credit to some, though not all, of the deletion-based gaming patches we observed, while still crediting genuine partial edits, so it may serve as a cheap screen before deeper, execution-based analysis. It is not a repair-quality metric, and we report where it falls short. Our findings argue for change-aware, execution-grounded evaluation of LLM-based vulnerability repair.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [321] [Not All 4-bit Quantizers Are Equal: Deployment-Time Mitigation of PII Leakage in Fine-Tuned Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25014)
*Cristhian Kapelinski,Diego Kreutz*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 4比特量化方法的选择会影响隐私：基于校准语料的方法（AWQ、GPTQ）几乎不泄露植入记录，而无校准的GGUF Q4_K_M格式复现了5.3%的植入记录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机构在私有数据上微调小语言模型后压缩到4比特以高效部署，作者关注到压缩方法本身可能影响隐私，而这一维度此前被视为仅关乎速度与质量。

Method: 在5个0.5-7B参数的开放模型上，对比两种基于校准语料的方法（AWQ、GPTQ）与无需校准语料的GGUF Q4_K_M格式；以每条植入记录自身的开头文本作为提示进行测试，并通过控制实验分析差异来源。

Result: 在主要模型上，AWQ与GPTQ均未复现任何植入记录，而GGUF Q4_K_M复现了5.3%；跨5个模型，AWQ在每个规模和两个模型家族中泄露最少，在3-7B规模下准确率损失很小；差异与校准引起的、涉及稀有词元预测通道的舍入误差相关。

Conclusion: 选择4比特量化方法是部署时的隐私决策，而不仅是速度与质量的问题。

Abstract: Organizations fine-tune small language models on private data and then compress them to 4 bits for resource-efficient deployment. We show that the compression method also affects privacy. What separates the methods is not the bit width but whether they tune their rounding on a small sample of text, the calibration corpus. On our primary model, when each planted record's own opening text is used as the prompt, the two calibration-based methods we test, Activation-aware Weight Quantization (AWQ) and Gradient-based Post-Training Quantization (GPTQ), each reproduce none of the planted records, while the calibration-corpus-free GGUF Q4_K_M format reproduces 5.3% of them. Tracked across five open models with 0.5-7 billion parameters, AWQ leaks least at every size and in both families, with little accuracy loss at 3-7 billion. Controlled experiments associate the difference with calibration-induced rounding error in channels involved in rare-token prediction. Choosing the 4-bit method is therefore a deployment-time privacy decision, not only a question of speed and quality.

</details>


### [322] [CPyGraph: A Version-Aware Static Analysis Framework for Native CPython Bytecode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25083)
*Baihong Chen,Wen Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CPyGraph:一个C++框架，用于对原生CPython字节码进行包级静态分析，跨版本恢复程序结构与对象流，并支持异常感知的CFG/CDG/DDG。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Python包的静态分析需要跨一等函数、动态分派、隐式协议调用、异常、闭包和模块执行恢复程序结构与对象流。原生CPython字节码提供了这些行为的可执行降级表示，但其指令、调用、栈和异常表示会随版本变化，因此需要一个版本感知的分析基础，使图产品共享相同的字节码身份与语义。

Method: 提出CPyGraph，一个用于包级分析原生CPython字节码的C++框架。版本特定适配器通过共享接口暴露栈、控制、调用、词法、协议和异常语义，同时保留代码对象身份和原生字节码偏移。操作数栈感知的Andersen指向分析与调用图共同增长至不动点。共享状态支持异常感知CFG、块级CDG和过程间DDG，并可选择函数级流、上下文和有界路径敏感性。框架还通过类型化覆盖摘要记录未解析的动态行为。

Result: 使用PYGBench评测，包含201个包级程序和1,733个固定候选项。在CPython 3.10上，默认分析达到91.40%候选精度和100%召回；完全敏感性达到94.97%精度且召回相同。在CPython 3.10-3.14间，1,334个版本不变查询中有1,328个一致，CPyGraph在其112程序调用图基准上与PyCG匹配。

Conclusion: CPyGraph为原生CPython字节码的包级分析提供了版本感知、共享字节码身份与语义的分析基础，在精度、召回和跨版本一致性上表现良好，并与PyCG在调用图基准上相当。

Abstract: Static analysis of Python packages must recover both program structure and object flow across first-class functions, dynamic dispatch, implicit protocol calls, exceptions, closures, and module execution. Native CPython bytecode provides the executable lowering of these behaviors, but its instruction, call, stack, and exception representations change across releases. This creates a need for a version-aware analysis foundation whose graph products share the same bytecode identities and semantics. We present CPyGraph, a C++ framework for package-level analysis of native CPython bytecode. Version- specific adapters expose stack, control, call, lexical, protocol, and exception semantics through a shared interface while preserving code-object identities and native bytecode offsets. An operand-stack-aware Andersen points- to analysis and call graph grow together to a fixed point. Their shared state supports exception-aware CFGs, block-level CDGs, and interprocedural DDGs, with optional function-level flow, context, and bounded path sensitivity. The framework also records unresolved dynamic behavior through typed coverage summaries. We evaluate CPyGraph with PYGBench, 201 package-level programs and 1,733 fixed candidates. On CPython 3.10, the default analysis reaches 91.40% candidate precision and 100% recall; complete sensitivity reaches 94.97% precision with the same recall. Across CPython 3.10-3.14, 1,328 of 1,334 version-invariant queries agree, and CPyGraph matches PyCG on its 112-program call-graph benchmark.

</details>


### [323] [Benchmarking Neural Defend ARCAS 1B: A Foundational Multimodal Deepfake Detection Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25154)
*Sivashankar Selvarajan,Piyush Verma,Sumit Kumar,Sharayu N. Deshmukh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本论文对 Neural Defend ARCAS 1B 检测器进行跨基准族评估，不做基准特定参数更新，保留原生聚合指标并补充记录级、覆盖率与子群诊断，强调可追溯的协议条件而非单一分数或排行榜比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 生成图像演进速度快于基准特定检测器评估，单一部分数无法完整反映泛化能力；需要更细致、可追溯的评估框架。

Method: 在多个基准族上评估 Neural Defend ARCAS 1B，不进行基准特定参数更新；保留原生聚合方式，并补充记录级度量、覆盖率核算与子群诊断；每个结果小节明确发布版本与评估总体，报告官方指标并描述错误模式；采用合并分析综合共性模式，同时区分原生量与合并量。

Result: 揭示了测试总体、类别平衡与缺失记录覆盖度等方面的差异；跨论文比较仅限于对齐证据，发布版本、总体、预处理、训练或基准暴露差异仅作为背景而非排名依据。

Conclusion: 研究结果刻画的是所评估记录上的表现，而非普适可靠性、校准、归因或未来自适应攻击下的稳健性；通过区分基准原生结果与合并摘要，支持在科研、平台安全与取证审查场景中解读检测器结果，强调可追溯协议条件优先于宣称或排行榜比较。

Abstract: AI-generated imagery evolves faster than benchmark-specific detector evaluations, making a single score an incomplete account of generalization. This paper evaluates Neural Defend ARCAS 1B across benchmark families without benchmark-specific parameter updates. We retain native aggregation and supplement it with record-level measures, coverage accounting, and subgroup diagnostics. Each Results subsection identifies the release and evaluation population, reports the official metric, and describes observed error patterns. A combined analysis synthesizes shared patterns while preserving the distinction between native and pooled quantities. Cross-paper comparisons are restricted to aligned evidence; differences in release, population, preprocessing, training, or benchmark exposure are context rather than rank. The findings characterize performance on evaluated records, not universal reliability, calibration, attribution, or future adaptive attacks. By keeping benchmark-native outcomes distinct from pooled summaries, the study makes test-population, class-balance, and missing-record-coverage differences visible. It supports interpretation of detector results in research, platform-safety, and forensic-review settings, foregrounding traceable protocol conditions over claims or leaderboard comparisons.

</details>


### [324] [Attack Success Rate Is Not a Number: On Measurement Validity in Agentic AI Security Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25173)
*Chetan Pathade,Prathamesh Pawar,Shubham Patil*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文指出LLM智能体安全评估中广泛使用的攻击成功率（ASR）并非单一指标，而是由六个设计选择参数化的一族指标，当前文献报告严重缺乏规范；作者通过元分析与分析性研究证明这种不可比性，并提出十项报告清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ASR几乎是所有LLM智能体攻击与防御评估的核心指标，但论文认为它并非单一量，而是由六个设计选择参数化的一族指标，这些选择在文献中很少被明确说明，也从未在不同研究间保持一致。这导致跨论文比较缺乏基础，而现有评估普遍忽视方差估计、重复运行、解码细节与评判一致性等关键信息。

Method: 研究包含两部分，均无需专有数据。第一，对2025年2月至2026年9月间arXiv上259篇智能体安全论文进行全文元分析，包括手工编码50篇随机样本与对全部259篇的自动编码，统计方差估计、重复运行、解码披露与LLM评判一致性检验的缺失情况。第二，在100个实例的基准上进行分析性研究，量化最小可检测ASR差异及单次运行评估导致防御排序错误的概率。

Result: 元分析显示，大多数论文对核心攻击指标既不报告方差估计也不报告重复运行：手工样本中58%（95% CI 44-71），全样本自动编码为65.3%；仅30.9%披露足够解码信息以判断评估是否随机；确认使用LLM评判的64篇中，29.7%报告了与人工标签的一致性检验。分析性研究表明，在100实例基准上，常规功效下可检测的最小ASR差异为18.2个百分点；真实ASR相差5个百分点的两个防御，单次运行评估约21%概率得出错误排序。

Conclusion: 由于六个维度对ASR的影响具有系统依赖性，其导致的不可比性并非可在比较中抵消的固定偏移，因此当前跨论文ASR比较缺乏支撑。作者提出十项报告清单，针对每一项测得的失败，旨在提供该领域此前缺失的共享测量契约，而非质疑任何单项结果。

Abstract: Attack success rate (ASR) is the headline metric in nearly every published evaluation of attacks on, and defenses for, LLM agents. We argue that ASR as currently used is not a single quantity but a family of metrics parameterized by six design choices that papers seldom specify and never hold constant across the literature. We support this with two studies that require no proprietary access. First, a full-text meta-analysis of 259 agentic-security papers posted to arXiv between February 2025 and September 2026 finds that most report neither a variance estimate nor repeated runs for their headline attack metric: 58% (95% CI 44-71) in a hand-coded random sample of 50, 65.3% by automated coding of all 259. Only 30.9% disclose enough about decoding to establish whether their evaluation was even stochastic, and of the 64 papers we confirm use an LLM judge, 29.7% report any agreement check against human labels. Second, an analytical study shows that these omissions are not cosmetic: on a 100-instance benchmark, the minimum difference in ASR detectable at conventional power is 18.2 percentage points, and two defenses whose true ASRs differ by 5 points are ranked in the wrong order by a single-run evaluation roughly 21% of the time. Because several of the six axes shift ASR in a system-dependent way, the resulting incomparability is not a constant offset that cancels in comparison. We conclude that cross-paper ASR comparison is currently unsupported, and propose a ten-item reporting checklist targeted at each failure we measure. Our aim is not to dispute any individual result but to supply the shared measurement contract the field has so far done without.

</details>


### [325] [Partition-Matched Evaluation of Community Features under Distribution Shift in Android Malware Function-Call Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25256)
*Junru Zhu,Yixin Yang,Xiaoqing Ding,Ruoyu Qi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该研究检验函数调用图中的中观社区结构是否比局部、全局、元数据及随机划分基线提供更稳定的跨分布迁移信号，结论是否定的：社区特征仅在IID场景有效，无重复可验证的shift稳定性优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于图的Android恶意软件分类器在恶意软件类型或家族分布发生偏移（shift）时会显著掉精度。作者希望验证函数调用图中的中观组织（mesoscopic organization，即社区结构）是否蕴含超出局部度数分布（LDP）、全局统计、轻量元数据以及规模匹配随机划分之外的、对分布偏移稳定的信息。

Method: 使用15,000个MalNet-Tiny、Common、Distinct图；在评估前预先指定六个Leiden社区描述符；采用五个优化器随机种子；对比社区特征与规模匹配随机划分、元数据等基线；用两级bootstrap置信区间评估shift退化差异，并做去除模块度的消融实验。

Result: 社区特征将IID下Tiny宏F1从78.7%提升至81.3%；但在仅源域训练的Common上退化29.4个点，而一个规模匹配随机划分退化27.8个点，五个随机划分平均退化27.9个点。随机减社区退化的95%两级bootstrap区间为[-4.2, 1.2]个点；加入元数据后差异为-0.2个点，区间[-1.1, 0.6]。去除模块度使仅结构Common宏F1从51.9%升至53.6%。

Conclusion: 所测试的中观特征仅带来IID信号的提升，未表现出可重复的shift稳定性优势。研究强调中观图结构的论断必须配合划分匹配的对照和重复零假设抽样，以避免将随机划分效应误读为社区结构的真实优势。

Abstract: Graph-based Android malware classifiers can lose accuracy under malware-type or family shifts. We test whether mesoscopic organization in function-call graphs provides shift-stable information beyond local degree profiles (LDP), global statistics, lightweight metadata, and size-matched random partitions. Using 15,000 MalNet-Tiny, Common, and Distinct graphs, six Leiden descriptors specified before evaluation, and five optimizer seeds, communities raise Tiny macro F1 from 78.7% to 81.3% but yield 29.4-point source-only Common degradation. One size-matched random partition yields 27.8-point degradation. Across five random partitions, mean degradation is 27.9 points; the 95% two-level bootstrap interval for random minus community degradation is [-4.2, 1.2] points. With metadata, the corresponding difference is -0.2 points with interval [-1.1, 0.6]. Removing modularity raises structure-only Common macro F1 from 51.9% to 53.6%. The tested signature adds IID signal but shows no repeatable shift-stability advantage, demonstrating why mesoscopic graph claims need partition-matched controls and repeated null draws.

</details>


### [326] [Controller-Only False Confirmation in Passive RF UAV Link Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25294)
*Rajendra Upadhyay,Rajendra Paudyal,Al Nahian Bin Emran,Duminda Wijesekera*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过双频段SDR测量实验，揭示仅测量"有链接状态vs环境背景"的准确率无法反映"仅有遥控器无飞行器"状态下的误确认率，并提出在显式约束下使用confirm/reject/defer三输出的检测方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有被动RF反无人机研究常报告高准确率，但这些结果多针对背景RF或WiFi/蓝牙干扰，很少单独隔离"仅有遥控器、未连接飞行器"这一实际威胁状态。作者认为仅凭有链接vs背景的准确率会高估系统性能，控制器单独工作时的误确认率应被单独报告。

Method: 使用两部USRP B210接收机同时扫描8个2.4 GHz和12个5.8 GHz观测窗口，覆盖三种商用UAV平台（DJI Phantom 3 4K、Hubsan H501S、DJI Mavic Mini），采集环境、仅遥控器、已链接三种状态共二十轮数据。先仅用已链接和环境数据训练基于能量的检测器，随后将仅遥控器数据纳入训练，并在显式的FCR约束下输出confirm、reject、defer三种判定。另进行硬件在环实验测量实际墙钟时间。

Result: 在四个排序并发驻留步（8 s）下，仅用已链接与环境训练的能量检测器达到0.992的平衡准确率，但在60次仅遥控器扫描中误确认为30次（FCRctrl=0.500）。纳入仅遥控器数据并施加FCR约束后，全平台汇总的FCRctrl从0.350降至0.050，但confirm-linked TPR从0.900降至0.400，且42.1%的扫描被延后判定；紧凑扫描对Hubsan和Mavic的效果显著优于Phantom。硬件在环实验将实测墙钟时间从82.9 s缩短至28.9 s。

Conclusion: 有链接状态相对于背景的准确率不能代表仅遥控器状态下的误确认率，这一误差必须单独报告；引入仅遥控器训练样本并在FCR约束下采用可延后输出，可在牺牲部分TPR和增加延后比例的前提下显著降低控制器误确认。

Abstract: Passive radio frequency (RF) sensing is widely used for counter-unmanned-aerial-vehicle (counter-UAV) detection. Existing studies commonly report high accuracy against background RF or WiFi/Bluetooth interference, but rarely isolate controller-only operation without a linked aircraft. We present a dual-band software-defined radio (SDR) measurement study with ambient, controller-only, and linked states for three commercial UAV platforms (DJI Phantom 3 4K, Hubsan H501S, DJI Mavic Mini). Two USRP B210 receivers simultaneously scan eight 2.4 GHz and twelve 5.8 GHz observation windows across twenty rounds. We first train an energy-based detector using linked and ambient scans only. At four ranked concurrent dwell steps (8 s), it achieves 0.992 linked-versus-ambient balanced accuracy but false-confirms 30 out of 60 controller-only scans (FCRctrl = 0.500). We then include controller-only scans in training and use confirm, reject, and defer outputs under an explicit controller-only false-confirmation-rate (FCR) constraint. For the pooled all-platform analysis, the constraint reduces observed FCRctrl from 0.350 to 0.050, while confirm-linked TPR decreases from 0.900 to 0.400 and 42.1% of scans are deferred. The corresponding compact scan performs substantially better for Hubsan and Mavic than for Phantom. A hardware-in-loop experiment reduces measured wall-clock time from 82.9 s to 28.9 s. These measurements show that linked-versus-background accuracy does not measure controller-only false confirmation and that this error should be reported separately.

</details>


### [327] [SSP-Bench: A Hybrid Data Generation Framework for Safety, Security, and Privacy Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25352)
*Fatih Deniz,Yazan Boshmaf,Issa Khalil*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出 SSP-Bench，一个针对安全、安保与隐私（SSP）领域的动态基准测试框架，可按需生成评估实例并保持领域一致性，揭示了静态基准在评估大语言模型时的系统性缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型在安全、安保与隐私（SSP）方面的评估高度依赖静态基准，但静态基准存在分数饱和、数据污染和聚合伪影等问题，且无法捕捉模型对语言变化的敏感性。因此，在固定测试集上表现良好的模型，在语义等价的重述下常常失效，亟需更贴近真实部署的评估方式。

Method: 提出动态基准框架 SSP-Bench：按需生成评估实例并保持领域一致性；通过外部权威来源确保标签有效性；利用特定服务验证约束范围；借助多模型引导面板校准难度；并将基准构建建模为针对难度、可分性、新颖性和多样性的多目标优化问题。

Result: 在 24 个模型和四种 SSP 服务上进行评估，SSP-Bench 揭示了静态评估的系统性失败，包括因构念混合导致的安全排名近乎零相关、安全性与过度拒答之间的强耦合，以及家族内隐藏的性能退化。

Conclusion: 静态基准可能错误呈现模型行为，这促使研究者转向动态、与部署相关的评估方法。

Abstract: Evaluation of large language models (LLMs) for safety, security, and privacy (SSP) relies heavily on static benchmarks, which suffer from score saturation, data contamination, and aggregation artifacts, and fail to capture sensitivity to linguistic variation. As a result, models that perform well on fixed test sets often fail under semantically equivalent rephrasings. We introduce SSP-Bench, a dynamic benchmarking framework that generates evaluation instances on demand while preserving domain consistency. The framework ensures label validity through externally grounded sources, enforces scope via service-specific validation, and calibrates difficulty using a multi-model steering panel. Benchmark construction is formulated as a multi-objective optimization problem over difficulty, separability, novelty, and diversity. Across 24 models and four SSP services, SSP-Bench reveals systematic failures of static evaluation, including near-zero correlation in safety rankings due to construct mixing, strong safety--over-refusal coupling, and hidden within-family regressions. These results show that static benchmarks can misrepresent model behavior, motivating dynamic, deployment-relevant evaluation.

</details>


### [328] [Quantum ROP: Using Quantum Algorithms for ROP Chain Selection in Exploit Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25364)
*Carlos Benitez*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该工作将ROP gadget选择建模为QUBO问题并用QAOA在真实IBM Heron r2硬件上求解，成功实现Linux内核提权，但在部分基准实例上因电路深度过大未能找到最优解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前量子计算对网络安全的威胁主要聚焦于Shor算法破解非对称密码学，而量子计算在攻击性安全中的其他潜在能力（如漏洞利用构造）尚未被充分探索。

Method: 将Return-Oriented Programming（ROP）的gadget选择问题形式化为二次无约束二进制优化（QUBO）问题，目标函数同时考虑单个gadget开销与gadget间寄存器破坏（register-clobbering）交互，并采用QAOA在真实IBM Heron r2量子硬件上求解。

Result: 在Linux内核漏洞利用场景中，QAOA选出的链在SMEP与SMAP均开启的情况下实现了提权至uid=0；在8个Linux二进制、16个基准实例中，QAOA在11个实例上找到了最低开销的有效链，其余5个未找到最优，失败与当前有限硬件上的电路深度过大有关。

Conclusion: 量子组合优化可应用于ROP gadget选择等攻击性安全任务，并在真实硬件上取得初步可行结果；但受限于现有量子硬件的电路深度，最优解恢复仍不完全稳定。

Abstract: The quantum computing threat to cybersecurity is nowadays predominantly framed around Shor's algorithm and its eventual capacity to break asymmetric cryptography. Beyond cryptanalysis, however, quantum computing may also enable other capabilities in offensive security. This work explores one such direction: the application of quantum combinatorial optimization to Return-Oriented Programming (ROP) gadget selection for exploit construction. We formulate gadget selection as a Quadratic Unconstrained Binary Optimization (QUBO) problem that captures individual gadget cost and inter-gadget register-clobbering interactions, and solve it using QAOA on real IBM Heron r2 hardware. Applied to a Linux kernel exploitation scenario, the QAOA-selected chain achieves privilege escalation to uid=0 with SMEP and SMAP active. Across eight Linux binaries and 16 benchmark instances, QAOA recovered the lowest-cost valid chain in 11 cases; in the remaining five, it did not recover the optimum, with the failures associated with excessive circuit depth on current limited hardware.

</details>


### [329] [Identifying Suspected Mislabeled Apps in Google Play Application Removal Prediction: An Empirical Comparison of Label Noise Detection Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25487)
*Deborah Dobles Montalvan,F. Mohsen,H. de Weerd*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该研究将Google Play应用下架预测中的标签噪声定义为"消失应用被标为removed但未记录原因"的失配问题，并利用三种不同类型的检测器识别出7,598个高置信度错标候选，发现剔除这些候选无法提升模型性能，但检测器的价值在于刻画标签噪声的双向错误模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预测应用下架/移除的模型依赖的标签仅记录某应用在后续观测时是否仍在商店中：消失即标为removed，存在即标为stable，但都不记录原因。自愿下架与政策下架都被统一标为removed，而未被发现的垃圾应用则保持stable。这种标签与实际下架原因之间的不一致构成了"标签噪声"，会误导模型学习。

Method: 采用来自不同方法论家族的三类检测器，应用于Mohsen、Karastoyanova和Azzopardi（2022）数据集中的870,514个应用：1）Isolation Forest，标记特征空间中异常的应用；2）Neighborhood Disagreement，标记最近邻具有相反标签的应用；3）Prediction Inconsistency，标记被分类器预测标签与数据标签不同的应用。取三者共同标记的重叠集合作为最强错标候选。

Result: 默认设置下三种检测器重叠标记7,598个应用。移除被标记应用并不能提升模型性能：没有任何检测器、重叠集合或并集能超过基线，且损失随移除数量增加而增大。经VirusTotal和Quark Engine确认的移除样本中，被标记应用的出现率低于预期，阈值收紧时降至预期率的0.43倍，而稳定侧的过量标记在考虑扫描应用年龄后消失。仅用3,021个可训练重叠应用训练的模型测试AUC仅为0.2518，远低于随机水平。

Conclusion: 被标记应用在两个方向上都是错误的：像垃圾应用一样被遗弃的应用带有stable标签，而看起来健康的应用却带有removed标签。检测器的价值不在于能有效清洗数据（无法获利于移除它们），而在于能够刻画这种标签噪声，定位出一小批无法通过移除来获益的候选样本。

Abstract: Models that predict which Google Play apps will be removed are trained on labels that record only whether an app was still in the store at a later observation. A disappeared app is labeled removed and a present one stable, but neither records why. A voluntary withdrawal and a policy takedown both produce removed, and an uncaught spam app keeps stable. This work calls that mismatch label noise. Three detectors from different methodological families are applied to the 870,514 apps of Mohsen, Karastoyanova, and Azzopardi (2022): Isolation Forest, flagging apps unusual in the feature space, Neighborhood Disagreement, flagging apps whose nearest neighbors carry the opposite label, and Prediction Inconsistency, flagging apps a classifier labels differently from the data. The apps flagged by all three, the overlap, number 7,598 at default settings and are the strongest mislabeling candidates. Two questions follow. First, does removing flagged apps improve the model? It does not. No detector, overlap, or union beats the baseline, and the loss grows with the number removed. Second, do flagged apps appear less often than expected among apps whose label VirusTotal and Quark Engine confirm? Among confirmed removals they do, falling to 0.43 times the expected rate as the threshold tightens, while an excess on the stable side disappears once the age of the scanned apps is accounted for. A model trained on only the 3,021 trainable overlap apps reaches a test AUC of 0.2518, far below chance, so the relationship between features and labels there runs opposite to the rest of the data. The flagged apps run wrong in both directions: abandoned apps that resemble spam carry stable, while apps that look healthy carry removed. The value of the detectors lies in characterizing this label noise. They locate a small set of candidates they cannot profitably remove.

</details>


### [330] [FASTAR: FRI Accelerator for Scalable Transparent ARguments of Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25535)
*Tengkai Gong,Xiaolin Xu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出FASTAR，一种面向FRI协议的FPGA加速器，采用约束驱动设计方法，通过HLS实现全参数化模块，可根据目标FPGA资源与内存约束自动生成硬件实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ZKP正从依赖可信设置且易受量子攻击的zk-SNARKs（如Groth16）转向透明、抗量子的zk-STARK，但后者仅依赖抗碰撞哈希函数，计算开销巨大。其中FRI协议主导了证明者复杂度，产生海量数据、重复的Merkle树承诺和不规则内存访问，限制了通用处理器上的性能与能效。

Method: 提出FASTAR——基于FPGA的FRI协议加速器。区别于在昂贵ASIC工艺节点上追求固定高性能内核的加速器，FASTAR采用约束驱动设计方法学，使用高层次综合（HLS）实现，由完全参数化的构建模块组成，覆盖FRI主要阶段：多项式求值、递归拆分折叠和Merkle树构建。根据用户提供的板卡规格，自动生成针对目标FPGA资源与内存约束定制的硬件实现。

Result: FASTAR能够根据用户提供的板卡规格自动生成适配目标FPGA资源与内存约束的硬件实现，支持无需人工重新设计即可部署到多种平台。

Conclusion: FASTAR通过约束驱动的参数化HLS设计，为FRI协议提供了可跨多种FPGA平台灵活部署的加速方案，缓解了zk-STARK证明者计算开销大的问题。

Abstract: Zero-Knowledge Proofs (ZKPs) enable a prover to cryptographically convince a verifier of the validity of a statement without revealing any underlying secrets, forming a foundational primitive for verifiable computation. The ZKP landscape is undergoing a fundamental shift from classic zk-SNARKs such as Groth16, which rely on trusted setup and are vulnerable to quantum adversaries, toward transparent, post-quantum constructions such as zk-STARK. These systems achieve post-quantum security by relying solely on collision-resistant hash functions, however, at the cost of substantial computational overhead. In particular, the Fast Reed--Solomon Interactive Oracle Proof of Proximity (FRI) protocol dominates prover complexity, generating massive data volumes, repeated Merkle-tree commitments, and irregular memory access patterns that limit performance and energy efficiency on general-purpose processors.
  To address these challenges, this work proposes FASTAR, a novel FPGA-based accelerator for the FRI protocol. Unlike accelerators that pursue fixed high-performance kernels on expensive ASIC process nodes, FASTAR adopts a constraint-driven design methodology. Our framework is implemented with High-Level Synthesis (HLS) and composed of fully parameterizable building blocks for the major stages of FRI, including polynomial evaluation, recursive split-and-fold, and Merkle-tree construction. From user-provided board specifications, FASTAR automatically generates hardware implementations tailored to the resource and memory constraints of the target FPGA, enabling deployment across a wide range of platforms without manual redesign.

</details>


### [331] [Rethinking Backdoor Repair Evaluation: Distinguishing Aggregate Clean Utility from Benign Performance Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25579)
*Baogang Song,Changtian Song,Jian Chen,Fan He,Junwei Zhou,Jianwen Xiang,Dongdong Zhao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文重新审视后门修复中的良性性能评估，指出总体干净准确率会掩盖局部类别性能退化，提出以类别级性能保持为核心的评估视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后门修复研究通常用攻击成功率（ASR）和总体干净准确率评估修复效果，但总体准确率可能掩盖集中在少数类别上的性能退化。作者希望从"性能保持"的角度重新审视良性性能评估，区分总体干净效用与原有类别级性能的保持情况。

Method: 作者定义类别级保持损失，通过比较修复前后的干净性能来衡量；揭示聚合指标可通过局部损失稀释和跨类补偿掩盖局部退化；引入最差类别保持损失（Worst-Class Preservation Loss）和尾部保持损失（Tail Preservation Loss）来刻画局部保持损失。并在代表性后门攻击、修复方法、数据集、攻击目标和模型架构上开展系统实证研究，并在干净标签攻击下做额外验证。

Result: 结果显示，有效的攻击抑制和良好的总体干净性能并不必然意味着各类别上原有良性性能得到一致保持；仍可能存在显著的局部保持损失，且其严重程度与类别结构因修复条件而异。

Conclusion: 这些发现表明，有必要在 ASR 和总体干净准确率之外，引入以保持为导向的类别级评估方法。

Abstract: Backdoor repair aims to suppress malicious behavior in compromised models while preserving benign task performance. Existing studies typically evaluate these objectives using Attack Success Rate (ASR) and Overall Clean Accuracy, but aggregate clean accuracy can obscure substantial degradation concentrated in a small portion of the label space. We revisit benign-performance evaluation from a preservation perspective by distinguishing aggregate clean utility from the preservation of previously available class-wise performance. We define class-wise preservation loss by comparing clean performance before and after repair and show that aggregation can hide localized degradation through localized-loss dilution and cross-class compensation. To complement Overall Clean Accuracy, we characterize localized preservation loss using Worst-Class Preservation Loss and Tail Preservation Loss. We conduct a systematic empirical study across representative backdoor attacks, repair methods, datasets, attack targets, and model architectures, with additional validation under clean-label attacks. Results show that effective attack suppression and favorable aggregate clean performance do not necessarily imply uniform preservation of previously available benign performance across classes. Substantial localized preservation losses can remain, and their severity and class-wise structure vary across repair conditions. These findings motivate preservation-oriented class-wise evaluation alongside ASR and Overall Clean Accuracy.

</details>


### [332] [SLED-IFV: Solver-Validated LLM-Guided Decomposition for Scalable Hardware Information-Flow Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25637)
*Liangtao Dai,Yimin Gao,Melika Morsali,Mircea Stan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文针对硬件信息流验证（IFV）在真实RTL上扩展性差的问题，提出两种语义证明分解形式（功能简化与关系强化），并构建了求解器验证的LLM引导流程SLED-IFV，在九个基准上实现最高603倍加速并将两个超时案例转为完成证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化硬件信息流验证能提供强保证，但在真实RTL上扩展性差。作者识别出self-composed IFV中的两类反复出现的证明障碍：一是实现复杂度，即证明困难的datapath逻辑占主导，而性质实际只需紧凑的边界关系；二是关系归纳复杂度，即证明依赖后端求解器无法高效推断的跨副本公共控制事实。

Method: 提出两种语义证明分解形式：功能简化，用经验证的过近似摘要替换证明困难的RTL区域；关系强化，暴露并证明归纳所需的跨副本关系。并实现SLED-IFV，一种求解器验证的LLM引导流程，自动选择这些形式及其具体目标。给定self-composed miter和无oracle的decision sheet，LLM提出分解方案，并在控制器检查下具体化为证明artifact；控制器将检查后的artifact编译为证明义务，形式验证后端作为唯一接受权威。

Result: 在由真实RTL构建的九个非平凡基准上，SLED-IFV实现最高603倍于纯求解器的加速，并将两个12小时超时转化为完成的证明。闭环流程为所有案例生成了验证器接受的分解。

Conclusion: 通过语义证明分解与LLM引导的自动化流程，可有效克服self-composed硬件信息流验证中的扩展性障碍，且形式验证后端仍保持唯一接受权威，保证了证明的可靠性。

Abstract: Formal hardware information-flow verification (IFV) provides strong guarantees against secret-dependent timing and control behavior, but often scales poorly on realistic RTL. We identify two recurring proof barriers in self-composed IFV: implementation complexity, where proof-hard datapath logic dominates even though the property needs only a compact boundary relation, and relational inductive complexity, where the proof depends on cross-copy public-control facts that the backend prover does not infer efficiently. To address them, we introduce two semantic proof decomposition forms: functional simplification, which replaces a proof-hard RTL region with a validated over-approximate summary, and relational strengthening, which exposes and proves the cross-copy relations needed for induction. We further present SLED-IFV, a solver-validated LLM-guided flow that automates the selection of these forms and their concrete targets. Given a self-composed miter and an oracle-free decision sheet, the LLM proposes a decomposition, then materializes it into proof artifacts under controller checks. The controller compiles the checked artifacts into proof obligations, and the formal verification backend remains the sole authority for acceptance. Across nine nontrivial benchmarks constructed from real RTL, SLED-IFV achieves up to 603x solver-only speedup and converts two 12-hour timeouts into completed proofs. The closed-loop flow produces verifier-accepted decompositions for all cases.

</details>


### [333] [C-to-Rust Fallacy: Automatic Refactoring != Memory Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25682)
*Hung-Mao Chen,Xu He,Bo Lu,Xiaokuan Zhang,Kun Sun*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对四种主流C到Rust自动重构工具进行了大规模实证评估，发现这些工具虽然减少了unsafe代码，但并未真正提升内存安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rust因内存与类型安全优势被视为C/C++的替代方案，但人工将C迁移到Rust需要深厚的领域知识，成本高昂。虽有C2Rust-analyze、CROWN等自动重构工具声称通过减少unsafe Rust实现安全，但这些工具与真正提升安全性之间的关联尚不明确，缺乏系统性的实证验证。

Method: 采用包含116个来自NIST Juliet测试套件的内存安全缺陷C程序作为数据集，使用C2Rust-analyze、CROWN、C2SaferRust和FLOURINE四种工具进行重构，共生成464个Rust程序。从三个方面评估：重构程序的编译正确性、对原始C缺陷的缓解效果、以及是否引入新的Rust缺陷。同时分析各工具设计理念及错误根因。

Result: 在464个生成的Rust程序中，342个无法编译；177个继承了原始C程序的内存安全缺陷；并新引入了77个Rust缺陷。各工具在不同错误类型上表现各异，工具设计理念与其实际安全效果存在明显偏差。

Conclusion: 当前的自动C-to-Rust重构工具仅实现了其自身定义范围内的内存安全（如减少unsafe代码），但采用这些工具时并不能保证更广泛意义上的内存安全。研究揭示了工具设计与实际安全目标之间的根本性差距。

Abstract: Rust has emerged as the leading system programming language, offering strong memory and type safety guarantees without compromising performance. This positions it as a compelling alternative to traditional languages like C and C++, which are susceptible to memory security bugs. However, manually transforming C to Rust requires in-depth domain knowledge of the Rust language features, which requires significant effort for developers. To address this, tools for automatic C-to-Rust refactoring aim to generate safe Rust code leveraging static analysis and Large Language Models (LLMs). While these tools claim to achieve safety by reducing the unsafe Rust, the correlation with improving security is not clear. In this paper, we conduct a comprehensive empirical study on the reliability, safety, and correctness of various C-to-Rust refactoring methods. Specifically, we evaluate C2Rust-analyze, CROWN, C2SaferRust, and FLOURINE using a dataset of 116 C programs with memory security bugs from the NIST Juliet Test Suite. Based on 464 Rust programs generated by these tools, our evaluation focuses on three key aspects: the compilation correctness of the refactored programs, the effectiveness in mitigating original C bugs, and the tendency to introduce additional Rust bugs. The results indicate that 342 Rust programs fail to compile, 177 Rust programs inherit memory security bugs from the original C programs, and 77 new Rust bugs are introduced. We examine the rationale behind tool design and analyze the root cause of errors across various refactoring methods. Our findings indicate that current automated refactoring tools deliver memory safety as they define it, but not the broader memory security when adopting them.

</details>


### [334] [GuidedRay: Diversity-Guided Direction Discovery for Targeted Hard-Label Black-Box Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25734)
*Fei Yuan,Yantian Shen,Qingyuan Yu,Yi Chen,Binghui Wang,Hongbo Yu,Anyu Wang,Xiaoyun Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出GuidedRay，一种基于多样性引导方向发现的目标化决策型黑盒对抗攻击方法，通过目标类参考样本生成多样候选方向并用单查询快速测试筛选，再用射线搜索缩减边界半径，从而显著降低初始化查询开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标化决策型黑盒攻击难度大：攻击者只能获取top-1标签，需在扰动受限下使模型预测指定目标类。在扰动精修前必须先找到一个指向目标区域的可行方向，而这一初始化步骤往往带来巨大的查询开销，现有方法在此阶段效率低。

Method: 基于两点观察：目标类参考样本可提供目标条件的方向先验；多样化候选能提高发现目标对抗方向的概率。GuidedRay从一个或多个目标类参考样本生成多样候选，使用单查询的Fast Test筛选其诱导的符号方向；一旦找到可行方向，则采用Ray Search缩减其决策边界半径。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和ImageNet上，GuidedRay在500至5,000四个查询预算下持续优于五种最先进的决策型攻击，尤其在初始化阶段的方向发现上提升显著；面对对抗训练或TRADES保护的模型，在四个查询预算下同样取得最高攻击成功率。

Conclusion: 通过多样性引导的方向发现与高效筛选，GuidedRay有效缓解了目标化决策型攻击的初始化查询瓶颈，在多种数据集和防御模型上均取得领先性能。

Abstract: Deep neural networks are vulnerable to adversarial attacks. Among black-box attacks, targeted decision-based attacks are particularly difficult: the attacker observes only the target model's top-1 label and aims to make it predict a prespecified target class under a bounded perturbation. Before perturbation refinement, the attacker must discover a direction that reaches the prescribed target region. This initialization step can incur substantial query cost. We propose GuidedRay, a targeted decision-based attack based on diversity-guided direction discovery. GuidedRay builds on two observations: target-class reference samples provide useful target-conditioned direction priors, and diverse candidates increase the probability of discovering a targeted adversarial direction. GuidedRay generates varied candidates from one or multiple target-class references and uses a one-query Fast Test to screen their induced sign directions. Once a feasible direction is found, GuidedRay applies Ray Search to reduce its decision-boundary radius. Experiments on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and ImageNet demonstrate that GuidedRay consistently outperforms five state-of-the-art decision-based attacks at four evaluated query budgets from 500 to 5,000, with particularly pronounced gains in direction discovery during initialization. Against models protected by adversarial training or TRADES, it likewise achieves the highest attack success rate at all four query budgets.

</details>


### [335] [Adaptive Traffic Camouflage: Causal and Resource-Aware Defense Against IoT Fingerprinting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25787)
*Daniel Adu Worae,Spyridon Mastorakis,Nuno Moniz,Nitesh V. Chawla*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出自适应流量伪装控制器，在不依赖运行时设备标签的情况下缓解IoT流量形状指纹识别，兼顾带宽与延迟开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加密虽隐藏IoT载荷，但数据包大小、时序、方向和分包方式等流量形状仍可泄露设备身份，需要一种因果、感知泄露且资源可控的伪装机制。

Method: 设计因果、泄露感知控制器，利用上一窗口上下文刻画流量形状泄露，从填充、分包、时序及复合变换中选取预算可行的方案，必要时不改动流量；在CIC-IoT-2022、IoT Sentinel和UNSW上，用经典与序列指纹模型在干净训练、防御感知和增量暴露设置下评估，并与固定、随机及均值带宽匹配基线对比。

Result: Balanced档下Macro-F1平均下降13.2-23.3%，平均带宽开销4.88-7.47%，附加延迟至多0.64 ms；Privacy档下降达28.0-43.5%。防御感知训练可恢复CIC-IoT-2022和UNSW上大部分攻击性能，但IoT Sentinel仍保留较大隐私差距；非因果同窗口参考相对上一窗口控制收益有限，元数据丰富攻击者在目标流量形状之外仍有效。

Conclusion: 因果、资源感知的伪装可在明确通信约束下降低IoT流量形状可指纹性，但保护持久性取决于被防御分布被学习的难易程度。

Abstract: Encryption hides IoT payloads, but traffic shape can still reveal device identity through packet sizes, timing, direction, and packetization. We present Adaptive Traffic Camouflage, a causal, leakage-aware controller that characterizes traffic-shape leakage without runtime device labels and selects a budget-feasible transformation for the next traffic window from previous-window context. The controller chooses among padding, packet splitting, timing, and composite transformations, or leaves traffic unchanged when camouflage is unnecessary. We evaluate the design on CIC-IoT-2022, IoT Sentinel, and UNSW using classical and sequence-based fingerprinting models under clean-trained, defense-aware, and incremental-exposure settings, with fixed, random, and mean-bandwidth-matched baselines. Under the Balanced profile, camouflage reduces mean Macro-F1 by 13.2-23.3% relative to clean traffic with 4.88-7.47% average bandwidth overhead and at most 0.64 ms added latency. Under the larger Privacy profile, the reduction increases to 28.0-43.5%. Defense-aware training recovers much of the lost attacker performance on CIC-IoT-2022 and UNSW, while IoT Sentinel retains a substantial privacy gap. A non-causal same-window reference provides only modest additional benefit over previous-window control, and metadata-rich attackers remain effective outside the targeted traffic-shape surface. These results show that causal, resource-aware camouflage can reduce IoT traffic-shape fingerprintability under explicit communication constraints, while the persistence of protection depends on how readily the defended distribution can be learned.

</details>


### [336] [On the Construction of Trapdoor Claw-Free Functions with Certifiable Key](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25819)
*Charles Lim,Yao Ma*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种与具体构造族无关的陷门无爪函数（TCF）密钥认证抽象，并据此将任意基于TCF的量子性证明编译为零知识证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TCF协议的安全性都相对于诚实生成的密钥来陈述，缺少对密钥本身合法性的通用认证机制；本文希望填补这一空白，并借此提升基于TCF协议的隐私性。

Method: 提出两个概念：可认证密钥关系（用NP关系刻画诚实密钥且见证可从陷门恢复）与认证密钥生成（每个密钥附带成员证书，满足完备性、可提取的证书可靠性及密钥隐私）；对不同构造实例化该关系，并通用地用零知识知识论证来满足它，再构造通用编译器。

Result: 通用编译器能把任意基于TCF的量子性证明转化为零知识版本，各项安全性质分别对应认证方案中的相应性质；同时证明在依赖单射不变性的协议中，被接受的证书本身即构成族区分器，会泄漏此类协议必须隐藏的比特。

Conclusion: 该抽象为TCF密钥认证提供了通用框架并扩展了零知识应用，但也划定了其能力边界：对单射不变性类协议，认证与隐私不可兼得。

Abstract: Trapdoor claw-free functions (TCFs) underpin much of classical-quantum cryptographic interaction, yet every TCF-based protocol states its guarantees relative to an honestly generated key. We give a family-agnostic abstraction of key certification for (noisy) TCF constructions, built on two notions: a certifiable key relation, an NP relation capturing a family's honest keys with witnesses recoverable from the trapdoor; and certified key generation, which emits with each key a certificate of membership satisfying completeness, certificate soundness with extractability, and key privacy. We instantiate certifiable key relations for different constructions, each met generically by a zero-knowledge argument of knowledge for the relation. As our main application, a generic compiler turns any TCF-based proof of quantumness into a zero-knowledge one, with each security property following from its counterpart in the certification scheme. Finally, we delimit the primitive's reach: for protocols resting on injective invariance, an accepting certificate is itself a family distinguisher, leaking exactly the bit such protocols must hide.

</details>


### [337] [COBRA: A Content-Agnostic Framework for Zero-Day Detection of Suspicious Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25882)
*Alexandros Fourtounis,Emmanouil Papadogiannakis,Panagiotis Papadopoulos,Nicolas Kourtellis,Evangelos Markatos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出COBRA框架，从域名注册时刻起、不依赖内容即可检测可疑恶意域名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恶意域名是钓鱼、恶意软件分发、冒充和欺诈交易等网络攻击的核心，注册成本低、易规模化部署，成为各行业网络犯罪最常见且危害大的工具之一。主动检测对缩短脆弱窗口期、保护用户至关重要，而现有方法多依赖内容特征，无法在域名刚注册、尚无内容时就识别。

Method: COBRA 是一种与内容无关、在注册时刻即检测的框架。它完全不依赖任何基于内容的特征，因此可在域名尚未填充内容前完成分类。方法上分析新注册域名的名称，采用聚类技术，依据词法与结构相似性对域名进行分组，从而识别和分析可疑域名。

Result: 在包含150万新创建域名的真实数据上评估，COBRA 检测可疑域名的精确率达98.5%，识别出超过47,000个不同的新注册可疑域名。结果还表明，域名名称聚类可实现准确早期检测，使80%的可疑或恶意域名比最广泛使用的威胁情报服务更早被发现，后者有时需长达7天。

Conclusion: 域名名称的词法与结构聚类无需内容即可在“零日”准确检测可疑域名，显著优于基于内容的方案，并大幅领先主流威胁情报服务的检测时效。

Abstract: The use of malicious domains is central to cyberattacks such as phishing, malware distribution, impersonation, and fraudulent transactions. Because domains are inexpensive to register and easy to deploy at scale, they remain one of the most common and damaging tools used in cybercrime across industries. Proactive detection is essential to reducing this window of vulnerability and preventing harm to users. In this work, we propose COBRA: a content-agnostic, registration-time detection framework for identifying and analyzing suspicious domains from day zero. Our approach does not rely on any content-based features, allowing us to classify a domain even before it is populated with content. We analyze the names of newly registered domains and employ a clustering technique to group them based on lexical and structural similarity. We evaluate our methodology using real-world data consisting of 1.5M newly created domains, demonstrating that COBRA detects suspicious domains with a precision of 98.5%, identifying more than 47K distinct newly registered suspicious domains. Furthermore, our results show that domain-name clustering enables accurate early detection, allowing us to identify 80% of suspicious or malicious domains earlier than one of the most widely used threat-intelligence services, which in some cases may require up to 7 days.

</details>


### [338] [LoRango: It Takes Two LoRAs to Unlock Hidden Behaviors in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25884)
*Jin Wei,Rundong Li,Ruihao Yang,Yikai Wang,Xiaoyuan Duan,Jianxiong Wu,Yanbo Wang,Chang Xu,Lingyun Zhang,Zhuyang Yu,Ping Chen,Jun Dai,Xiaoyan Sun*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文发现并实现了文本到图像扩散模型中一种“配对条件攻击”：单个LoRA适配器看似安全有用，但与特定匹配伙伴共同加载时会触发恶意生成，且无需修改提示词或基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户常组合多个LoRA适配器来个性化图像（不同主体、风格、属性），但单独检查每个适配器无法保证其组合的安全性，因此需要研究多LoRA组合的安全风险。

Method: 提出LoRango攻击，由互补的Signature与Payload适配器组成。Signature在中间载体表示中写入配对特定代码，Payload利用代码选择性响应及相反的信号/参考分支；对单个或不匹配配对，两分支近似抵消，而匹配的代码-读取器对齐会在原生GEGLU块内打破抵消，释放预设动作。两者均导出为普通静态LoRA文件，兼容标准加载器，无需提示词触发或基础管线修改。

Result: 在SD v1.5上匹配配对攻击成功率达97.9%，SDXL上达98.7%；而单独加载被植入适配器时仅2.8–4.6%。实验还评估了配对选择性、单独保真度、部署变化鲁棒性及跨去噪器架构适用性。

Conclusion: 单独检查适配器不足以评估多LoRA个性化的安全性，需对适配器组合进行审计。

Abstract: Users commonly combine multiple Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) adapters to personalize images with different subjects, styles, and visual attributes. Yet inspecting adapters individually does not establish the safety of their composition. We identify and characterize a pair-conditioned attack in text-to-image diffusion: individually useful and benign-appearing adapters redirect image generation when co-loaded with a specifically matched partner, whose identity serves as the trigger. We introduce LoRango to realize this attack through complementary Signature and Payload adapters. The Signature writes a pair-specific code into intermediate carrier representations, while the Payload uses code-selective responses and opposing signal/reference branches. These branches approximately cancel for standalone adapters and mismatched pairs; matched code-reader alignment breaks cancellation within native GEGLU blocks and releases the programmed action. Both adapters are exported as ordinary static LoRA files compatible with standard loaders, requiring no prompt trigger or base-pipeline modification. LoRango achieves matched-pair attack success rates of 97.9\% on SD v1.5 and 98.7\% on SDXL, compared with 2.8--4.6\% when implanted adapters are loaded individually. Further experiments evaluate pair selectivity, standalone fidelity, robustness to deployment variations, and applicability across denoiser architectures. These findings show that individual-adapter inspection is insufficient to assess the security of multi-LoRA personalization and motivate auditing adapter compositions.

</details>


### [339] [How It's Made: Uncovering Detection Engineering Processes for Network Intrusion Detection Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25901)
*Koen T. W. Teuwen,Emmanuele Zambon,Luca Allodi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过引入 SuriCap 平台并举办 CTF 式研讨会，研究基于签名的网络入侵检测系统（如 Suricata）的检测规则工程过程，分析了 60 名参与者生成的 3146 条有效规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全运营中心（SOC）广泛依赖基于签名的网络入侵检测系统（如 Suricata），但其检测规则工程流程仍缺乏深入研究。

Method: 提出 SuriCap 平台用于规则工程练习，并举办 CTF 式研讨会；60 名参与者（受训硕士生与经验丰富的 SOC 专业人员）针对四个场景创建规则，共产生 3146 条有效规则，据此分析其方法、性能与迭代模式。

Result: 出乎意料的是，先前经验对规则质量影响有限，表明经验较少者也能产出与专家相当的规则；同时观察到规则难以泛化到测试之外，凸显足量标注数据的重要性。

Conclusion: 研究识别出规则工程的三个阶段及一个常见模式，为 SOC 管理者改进流程、调整对工程师专业能力的预期提供了参考洞见。

Abstract: Many Security Operations Centers rely on signature-based Network Intrusion Detection Systems like Suricata, yet detection rule engineering remains understudied. We investigate this process by introducing SuriCap, a platform for rule engineering exercises, and hosting CTF-style workshops where 60 participants, trained MSc students, and experienced SOC professionals, created rules for four scenarios. Participants produced 3146 valid rules, enabling analysis of their methods, performance, and iteration patterns. Surprisingly, prior experience had limited impact on rule quality, suggesting that less experienced engineers can produce rules comparable to experts. We also observed challenges in generalizing rules beyond available tests, underscoring the need for sufficient labeled data. From our study, we identify three phases and a common pattern in rule engineering, offering SOC managers insights to improve their processes and expectations of engineer expertise.

</details>


### [340] [Toward Responsible AI-Augmented Cyber Defense: Pattern Recognition, Defense-in-Depth, and the Case for Human-AI Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25921)
*Mustafa S. Aljumaily,Hayder Kareem Abed,Nawar S. Alseelawi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个可证伪的形式化模型，将纵深防御理论、AI模式识别理论与安全运营中的人机协作统一为伯努利检测级联与容量受限分诊模型，并通过蒙特卡洛/解析仿真表明：AI增强在纵深防御各层间具有复利效应，且对全部AI告警进行人工复核并非最优，存在一个内部最优的容量比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络安全文献虽已记录AI在威胁检测、事件响应与预防方面的运营收益，并提出了过度自动化、算法偏见和分析师技能退化等定性担忧，但缺乏一个形式化、可证伪的模型，将反复出现的三大构念（纵深防御理论、AI模式识别理论、安全运营中的人机协作）联系起来，因此"平衡的人机协作"这一建议始终停留在定性层面，缺乏精确、可检验的表述。

Method: 将分层防御形式化为伯努利检测级联，AI增强以乘法方式进入各层；将每层的模式识别行为形式化为具有闭式最优阈值的Neyman-Pearson/贝叶斯检测器；将人机分诊形式化为容量受限级联，显式量化检测概率与误报率之间的权衡。采用蒙特卡洛与解析仿真，在示例性但贴近现实的运行点进行评估。

Result: 仿真显示：(i) AI增强在防御层间产生复利效应，其最大边际收益恰好出现在传统分层趋于饱和之处；(ii) 对AI标记告警进行100%人工复核并非最优——将分析师覆盖率提升至接近100%可使误报减少约20倍，但同时降低系统级检测概率，因为此时不完美的人工准确率被施加于每一个告警而非经过滤的子集之上。

Conclusion: 本研究为"平衡的人机协作"这一广为重复的定性建议赋予了精确、可检验的形式，并提出内部最优容量比可作为安全运营中心（含保护IT/OT融合关键基础设施者）的具体设计目标。

Abstract: Cybersecurity literature has extensively documented the operational benefits of artificial intelligence (AI) for threat detection, incident response, and prevention, while raising qualitative concerns about over-automation, algorithmic bias, and analyst-skill erosion. What remains largely absent is a formal, falsifiable model connecting three constructs that recur across this literature: Defense-in-Depth Theory, the Artificial Intelligence Theory of Pattern Recognition, and human-AI collaboration in security operations. This paper develops such a model. We formalize layered defense as a Bernoulli detection cascade in which AI augmentation enters multiplicatively across layers; we formalize each layer's pattern-recognition behavior as a Neyman-Pearson/Bayesian detector with a derived closed-form optimal threshold; and we formalize human-AI triage as a capacity-constrained cascade with an explicit, quantifiable trade-off between detection probability and false-alarm ("alert fatigue") rate. A Monte Carlo/analytical simulation evaluated at illustrative but realistic operating points shows that (i) AI augmentation compounds across defense layers, delivering its largest marginal gains exactly where traditional layering saturates, and (ii) full human review of AI-flagged alerts is not optimal: increasing analyst capacity toward 100% coverage cuts false alarms by roughly 20-fold but simultaneously lowers system-level detection probability, because imperfect analyst accuracy is then applied to every alert rather than a filtered subset. These results give the widely repeated qualitative recommendation of "balanced human-AI collaboration" a precise, testable form and suggest an interior-optimum capacity ratio as a concrete design target for security operations centers (SOCs), including those securing IT/OT-converged critical infrastructure.

</details>


### [341] [Policy-Backed Selective Regeneration under Tainted Inter-Agent Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26072)
*Jinghan Xu,Longze Fan,Zeyuan Wang,Xinjin Li,Hankai Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出ESC-CR框架，通过将消息内容与授权分离、在外部释放边界强制执行可执行语义承诺，并在违规时进行清洁室恢复，从而在保留有用信息的同时抑制多智能体系统中的未授权指令。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体语言模型系统中，单条消息可能混合任务关键信息与未经原始请求授权的指令。基于提示的防御将执行责任交给暴露于对抗消息的模型，而一刀切地删除整条消息又会丢失有用信息，因此需要一种既安全又不浪费信息的机制。

Method: 提出ESC-CR（Executable Semantic Commitments with Clean-Room Recovery）策略支持的框架。它将消息声明与授权分离，基于可信任务、证据与策略构建可执行承诺，并在外部释放边界强制执行。一旦违规，对责任消息与被拒产物进行标记，从有证据支撑的任务信息重建干净上下文，并在同一策略下重新生成。

Result: 在通信必需与标准代码生成基准、多种模型族与通信拓扑、以及涵盖直接、混淆与验证器感知载荷的自适应攻击上评估。结果显示，污染上下文重试往往无法移除未授权影响，而完全删除消息会丢弃通信必需任务所需的信息。在与计算预算匹配的条件下，ESC-CR能保留有证据支撑的声明并抑制未授权释放，且同一设计可迁移到端到端智能体轨迹。

Conclusion: ESC-CR提供了一种在外部边界强制执行授权、并在违规时进行清洁恢复的安全机制，在保留有用信息的同时有效抑制未授权指令，且具备跨模型、拓扑与任务的通用性。

Abstract: Inter-agent communication is essential to multi-agent language-model systems, yet a single message may combine task-critical information with instructions not authorized by the original request. Prompt-based defenses leave enforcement to models exposed to adversarial messages, while indiscriminate message removal discards useful information. We introduce Executable Semantic Commitments with Clean-Room Recovery (ESC-CR), a policy-backed framework for secure inter-agent code generation and recovery. It separates message claims from authorization, constructs executable commitments from trusted tasks, evidence, and policy, and enforces them at an external release boundary. Upon a violation, ESC-CR taints the responsible message and rejected artifact, reconstructs a clean context from evidence-backed task information, and regenerates under the same policy. We evaluate ESC-CR across communication-essential and standard code-generation benchmarks, multiple model families and communication topologies, and adaptive attacks spanning direct, obfuscated, and verifier-aware payloads. Results show that polluted-context retry frequently fails to remove unauthorized influence, while complete message removal can discard information required by communication-essential tasks. ESC-CR preserves evidence-backed claims while suppressing unauthorized releases under matched computational budgets, and the same design transfers to end-to-end agent trajectories.

</details>


### [342] [Learning to Link: Automatic Re-identification of BLE Devices Under MAC Address Randomisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26079)
*Reem Abdulrhman Alghamdi,Alberto Verna,Marco Mellia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究利用机器学习自动化追踪BLE设备，即使其使用MAC地址随机化（RPA）。基于真实BLE广告流量，将设备关联建模为监督分类问题，验证广告层元数据特征在RPA变化下的持续性，并用决策树分类器实现设备重识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BLE通过可解析私有地址（RPA）实现MAC地址随机化，以缓解公共广告信道上的长期设备追踪。然而已有研究表明广告包中的元数据和结构特征可被人工规则用于重识别设备。本文旨在探究是否可以利用机器学习算法自动化这一追踪过程，从而降低攻击门槛。

Method: 基于真实BLE目标设备的广告流量，刻画广告层特征在RPA变化下的持续性，将设备关联问题形式化为监督分类任务。采用简单的决策树分类器作为可行性验证手段，评估在不同地址轮换模式下目标与非目标设备的可区分性，且无需对BLE技术的专门知识。

Result: 结果表明，广告层元数据能够在MAC随机化条件下实现设备重识别，且该关联过程可通过标准监督学习技术自动化完成，无需特定技术知识。

Conclusion: 本研究证实了MAC随机化下广告层元数据导致设备可被重识别的风险，并进一步表明借助标准机器学习方法即可自动化实现设备追踪，突显了BLE隐私保护机制的局限性。

Abstract: Bluetooth Low Energy (BLE) employs MAC address randomisation -- via Resolvable Private Address (RPA) -- to mitigate long-term device tracking on public advertising channels. Existing research has shown that advertising packets contain metadata and structural features that allow re-identifying a target device via manually crafted rules. In this work, we investigate the feasibility of automating the process of tracking BLE devices despite MAC randomisation by leveraging machine learning algorithms for the signature creation. Based on the actual Bluetooth traffic from target devices, we characterise the persistence of advertising-layer features across RPA changes and formulate device linkage as a supervised classification problem. Using simple decision tree classifiers as a proof-of-feasibility approach, we evaluate the distinguishability of target and non-target devices under varying address rotation patterns. Our results reinforce prior work demonstrating that advertising-layer metadata can enable device re-identification under MAC randomisation, to the point where such linkage can be automated using standard supervised learning techniques, without any specific knowledge of the technology.

</details>


### [343] [From Bilinear to Linear: Differentially Private Federated LoRA via Low-Dimensional Parameterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26091)
*Lele Zheng,Ruijie Hu,Tao Zhang,Ke Cheng,Yulong Shen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出FedHSIP，一种基于统一低维参数化的差分隐私联邦LoRA框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦LoRA虽避免共享原始数据，但传输的模型更新仍存在隐私泄漏风险。引入差分隐私(DP)后会产生两个根本挑战：低秩因子独立平均导致的聚合不匹配，以及同时向两个因子注入噪声引起的二次噪声放大。

Method: FedHSIP将所有LoRA参数重构为一个共享的低维可训练向量，使客户端仅优化和通信低维更新；该重构把联邦LoRA从双线性因子聚合问题转化为统一线性参数空间，从而消除聚合不匹配并防止DP噪声的二次放大。针对非IID数据，提出由预热统计构建的异构性与敏感性感知的等距投影，将跨客户端更新模式兼容的坐标分组，同时平衡低维空间中的敏感性、更新能量与异构性。

Result: 在自然语言理解与生成基准上，FedHSIP在私有和非私有设置下均持续优于现有联邦LoRA方法；在差分隐私下最高提升3-4%，通信成本降低超过80%，并在异构数据分布下保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: FedHSIP通过统一低维参数化有效解决了联邦LoRA与差分隐私结合时的聚合不匹配和噪声放大问题，并通过异构性与敏感性感知投影应对非IID数据，在性能、通信效率和鲁棒性方面均取得显著优势。

Abstract: Federated Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) provides an efficient solution for finetuning large language models across distributed and privacy-sensitive data. However, despite avoiding raw data sharing, federated LoRA remains vulnerable to privacy leakage through transmitted model updates. Differential privacy (DP) mitigates such leakage, but integrating DP into federated LoRA introduces two fundamental challenges: aggregation mismatch from independently averaging low-rank factors, and quadratic noise amplification when noise is injected into both factors. To address these challenges, we propose FedHSIP, a differentially private federated LoRA framework based on a unified low-dimensional parameterization. FedHSIP reformulates all LoRA parameters into a shared low-dimensional trainable vector, enabling clients to optimize and communicate only low-dimensional updates. This reformulation transforms federated LoRA from a bilinear factor aggregation problem into a unified linear parameter space, thereby eliminating aggregation mismatch and preventing the quadratic amplification of DP noise. To further handle non-IID data, we introduce a heterogeneity- and sensitivity-aware isometric projection, constructed from warm-up statistics, which groups coordinates with compatible cross-client update patterns while balancing sensitivity, update energy, and heterogeneity across the low-dimensional space. Extensive experiments on natural language understanding and generation benchmarks show that FedHSIP consistently outperforms existing federated LoRA methods under both private and non-private settings, achieving up to 3-4% improvements under differential privacy while reducing communication cost by over 80% and maintaining robustness under heterogeneous data distributions.

</details>


### [344] [Beyond Classification Accuracy: Quantifying Fingerprint Complexity in Encrypted Darknet Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26096)
*Javeriah Saleem,Rafiqul Islam,Md Zahidul Islam*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出指纹复杂度分数（FCS）框架，用于量化暗网服务指纹的内在复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有暗网流量研究主要通过分类性能评估服务指纹可识别性，难以解释为何某些服务更易或更难识别，缺乏对内在原因的深入洞察。

Method: 提出FCS框架，基于行为重叠、不确定性、不一致性与持续混淆四个维度量化暗网服务指纹的内在复杂度；在Tor、I2P、FreeNet、ZeroNet四个匿名网络的25个服务上开展实验，并使用随机森林、Extra Trees、XGBoost进行验证。

Result: 指纹复杂度在不同服务间差异显著，行为重叠是主要贡献因素；指纹复杂度与识别性能呈强负相关（Pearson r = -0.706，Spearman ρ = -0.765，p < 0.001）。

Conclusion: 服务指纹可识别性根本上由行为复杂度决定，为分析匿名网络中的行为信息泄漏提供了新视角。

Abstract: Existing darknet traffic studies primarily evaluate service fingerprintability through classification performance, providing limited insight into why certain services are easier or harder to identify. This paper introduces the Fingerprint Complexity Score (FCS), a framework for quantifying the intrinsic complexity of darknet service fingerprints using behavioral overlap, uncertainty, disagreement, and persistent confusion. Experiments on 25 services across the Tor, I2P, FreeNet, and ZeroNet anonymity networks reveal substantial variation in fingerprint complexity, with behavioral overlap emerging as the dominant contributor. Validation using Random Forest, Extra Trees, and XGBoost demonstrates a strong inverse relationship between fingerprint complexity and recognition performance (Pearson r = -0.706, Spearman \r{ho} = -0.765, p < 0.001). The findings show that service fingerprintability is fundamentally governed by behavioral complexity, providing a new perspective for analyzing behavioral information leakage in anonymity networks.

</details>


### [345] [A Cross-Dataset based Zero-Day Intrusion Detection System by Integrating Siamese Network and Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26115)
*Md. Meheraj Hossain,Saumik Das Turja,Sibgatullah Tasnim,Md. Fahmid-Ul-Alam Juboraj,Muhammad Iqbal Hossain*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合孪生网络异常关联与强化学习自适应防御的混合式零日入侵检测系统，用于IoT网络安全，实现高精度未知攻击与零日威胁检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零日威胁在IoT网络安全中不断涌现，传统入侵检测机制难以在复杂IoT环境中泛化动态零日漏洞，因此需要具备认知检测能力的机制来识别新兴恶意行为。

Method: 采用基于孪生网络的异常关联与基于强化学习的自适应防御相结合的混合方法；使用无监督机器学习分类器在基准IoT数据集上，通过基于距离的相似性分析检测未知异常；引入基于近端策略优化（PPO）的强化学习智能体，借助持续反馈与优化动态调整防御策略。

Result: 实验评估显示训练准确率达99.28%，未知攻击检测准确率为99.07%，零日检测率为93.94%，验证了模型在数据集上的收敛性与稳定性。

Conclusion: 该系统通过平衡精度、延迟与误报率，提供了一种自学习、可扩展的IoT防御机制，为下一代自主网络安全方案应对不断变化的零日威胁奠定了基础。

Abstract: Zero-day threats are nascent for the Internet of Things (IoT) network security, which demands cognitive detec-tion mechanisms that can identify emerging malicious behavior. Conventional intrusion detection mechanisms fail to generalize dynamic zero-day exploits within sophisticated IoT environments. This paper proposes a hybrid zero-day intrusion detection system using Siamese network-based anomaly correlation and reinforcement learning-based adaptive defense. Furthermore, the paper uses unsupervised machine learning classifiers over benchmark IoT datasets with the intention of detection of known attack types compared to unknown anomalies using distance-based similarity analysis to detect possible zero-day attacks. To facilitate adaptability, a Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) reinforcement learning-based agent dynamically adjusts the defense policy with continuous feedback and optimization. Experimental evaluations demonstrate 99.28% training accuracy, 99.07% accuracy in unknown attack detection, and 93.94% zero-day detection ratio, confirming the convergence and stability of the model on datasets.The system offers a self-learning and extensible defense mechanism of IoT deployments by finding the right balance between precision, latency and false positives. This deep anomaly correlation with adaptive reinforcement learning is a firm base on which the next generation and autonomic cyber security solutions can take the reins as the zero-day threats keep changing their course.

</details>


### [346] [Zeta-Transform Evaluation for Higher-Order Vanishing Key Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26132)
*Sunyeop Kim,Insung Kim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文利用截断的加权上zeta变换加速Hemmert针对Classic McEliece的高阶消失密钥恢复算法，在不改变算法框架的前提下降低核计算中重复矩阵-向量乘的代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hemmert的密钥恢复算法中，计算ker(φ̃_A^(p))的核是Classic McEliece参数下的主导开销，因此需要降低这一核计算的成本。

Method: 将定义φ̃_A^(p)的求和按列解释为布尔格上的加权上zeta变换；由于φ̃_A^(p)只需变换的选定层，将求值限制在第p层到最低需求层之间的带状区域，从而精确求得φ̃_A^(p)及其转置；再将截断zeta变换用于Wiedemann核计算。

Result: 对五个Classic McEliece参数集，在all-one模型下估计的密钥恢复成本降低14.09–41.19比特，在Bernoulli(1/2)模型下降低7.25–22.48比特。

Conclusion: 截断加权上zeta变换可有效降低核计算中重复矩阵-向量乘的代价，且不改变整体密钥恢复算法；精确成本取决于H'''非主元列的权重分布。

Abstract: Hemmert's key-recovery algorithm for Classic McEliece is based on higher-order vanishing. It computes a basis of $\ker(\widetilde\varphi_A^{(p)})$, where $A=H'''$ is the shortened parity-check matrix used in the attack. For Classic McEliece parameters, this kernel computation is the dominant cost of the attack. We show that the sums defining $\widetilde\varphi_A^{(p)}$ can be evaluated, column by column, as weighted upper zeta transforms on the Boolean lattice. Since only selected levels of these transforms are required by $\widetilde\varphi_A^{(p)}$, restricting their evaluation to the band between level $p$ and the lowest required level yields exact evaluations of both $\widetilde\varphi_A^{(p)}$ and its transpose. Using the resulting truncated zeta-transform evaluation in the Wiedemann-based kernel computation reduces the cost of the repeated matrix--vector products without changing the overall key-recovery algorithm. The exact cost depends on the weight distribution of the non-pivot columns of $H'''$.
