We therefore consider two models: an all-one model, in which every relevant binary coordinate is active, and a Bernoulli$(1/2)$ model, in which the coordinates are independently active with probability $1/2$. For the five Classic McEliece parameter sets, our method reduces the estimated key-recovery cost by $14.09$--$41.19$ bits in the all-one model and by $7.25$--$22.48$ bits in the Bernoulli$(1/2)$ model.

</details>


### [347] [The Uncontrolled Variable: Vision-Language Model Refusal Responds to Image Presence in Ways Risk Cannot Explain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26174)
*Haoyu Zhang,Yi Feng,Shibo Zheng,Zhuoxi Wang,Xiao Luo,Haowen Xu,Xiangchen Guan,Mohammad Zandsalimy,Shanu Sushmita*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 安全对齐的视觉语言模型（VLM）在拒答决策上不仅依赖请求的实质内容，还依赖请求的形式特征——是否附带图像。附加一张空白画布即可使拒答率发生数十个百分点的偏移，且该效应集中于隐私、自残、暴力等敏感邻近话题，无法被指令消除，代价远超收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有观点认为VLM的安全性应取决于请求"问的是什么"。作者意在揭示一个被忽视的安全维度：拒答行为还取决于请求的"形式"（是否附带图像），即图像存在性本身就是一个未被部署者选择或计量的不受控变量。

Method: 在保持请求语义完全不变的前提下，向提示附加一张不可读、与请求无关、跨提示完全相同的空白画布，观察拒答率变化；对比中性指令与边缘良性提示的差异；比较黑白画布、像素数量等图像属性影响；测试指令显式声明"图像为占位符请忽略"以及无图像时声称存在附件的效果；在多个开放权重检查点及不同服务栈上复现。

Result: 附加空白画布使拒答率偏移数十个百分点；中性指令几乎不受影响，而边缘良性提示（隐私、自残、暴力话题）显著移动。仅"附件存在"即足以触发，图像属性决定代价大小：黑色画布代价远高于同尺寸白色画布，某一开放检查点上的主导轴是像素数量。指令控制作用有限，声明占位符仅能消除一小部分偏移。该效应非服务栈差异（同一权重两种途径行为一致），非VLM共性（多个开放权重检查点无此现象），而属于特定对齐检查点（含一个开放模型）。与收益脱钩：画布虽能阻断部分匹配有害集的攻击成功，但远小于其代价，且方向不固定——在某开放模型上同一画布反而使攻击更容易。

Conclusion: 图像存在性并非部署者主动选择或计价的默认设置，而是随权重一并继承的、不受控的变量。VLM拒答既未追踪风险，也未受指令控制，构成对"对齐模型以请求内容为安全依据"这一假设的实质性挑战。

Abstract: Vision-Language Model (VLM) safety is expected to depend on what a request asks for. We show that safety-aligned VLMs also key refusal on a property of a request's form: whether an image is attached, holding everything the request asks fixed. Attaching a blank canvas - unreadable, unrelated to the request, identical across prompts - shifts refusal by tens of percentage points, with no defense in the loop.
  The shift is not blanket caution. Neutral instructions are almost unaffected while borderline-benign prompts move sharply, so the cost falls on sensitivity-adjacent traffic: benign questions about privacy, self-harm and violence. Attachment alone is sufficient, while the image's properties set the price: a black canvas costs substantially more than a white one of identical size, and on an open checkpoint the carrying axis is pixel count. Nor is the shift under instructional control - telling the model the image is a placeholder to be disregarded removes only a fraction of it, and on one model asserting that an attachment exists moves refusal substantially with nothing attached.
  Attachment may correlate with risk in deployment; what these models do with it does not track risk. It is not the serving stack, since the same weights reached two ways behave alike, nor a property of VLMs as such, since several open-weight checkpoints show nothing. It belongs to particular aligned checkpoints, one of them open. It is also decoupled from what it buys: the canvas does prevent some attack success on a matched harmful set, but far less than it costs, and its sign is not fixed - on one open model the identical canvas makes the model markedly easier to attack. Image presence is not a default a deployer chose or priced; it is an uncontrolled variable inherited with the weights.

</details>


### [348] [Towards Effective Black-Box Adversarial Attacks on Deep Code Models via Structural and Identifier Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26234)
*Bin Duan,Jintao Lin,Dan Dongseong Kim,Guowei Yang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出Strike，一种输入条件下的黑盒对抗鲁棒性测试框架，通过构建并搜索分层扰动空间生成对抗样本，在多个代码智能任务上取得更高攻击成功率，且生成样本自然、可提升模型跨攻击鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度代码模型在代码智能任务中应用日益广泛，但其对抗鲁棒性认识不足。已有黑盒攻击多依赖仅标识符替换或从参考样本迁移的结构编辑，扰动空间基本独立于被攻击输入，缺乏针对性与有效性。

Method: Strike为每个输入构建并搜索分层扰动空间：先将代码划分为块，用LLM生成上下文相关的结构候选，经语法有效性过滤、相似度排序并自适应组合；再对最佳结构变体通过相似度引导的标识符替换进行精炼，候选池动态构建。

Result: 在克隆检测、漏洞检测、代码摘要等任务上，Strike获得更高攻击成功率，目标模型查询开销具有竞争力。策略特定静态检查与可执行Juliet漏洞检测子集的执行验证提供任务受限的有效性证据；表示相似度与人工评估表明变体与原始代码高度相似且上下文自然；使用Strike样本微调可提升固定扰动评估集上的跨攻击性能并保持干净性能。

Conclusion: Strike验证了输入条件下分层扰动搜索在黑盒对抗测试中的有效性，生成对抗样本兼具攻击效果与自然性，并可用于微调以增强模型跨攻击鲁棒性。

Abstract: Deep code models (DCMs) are increasingly embedded in code intelligence tasks. However, their robustness under adversarial attacks remains insufficiently understood. Prior black-box attacks mainly rely on identifier- only substitutions or structural edits transferred from reference samples, yielding perturbation spaces defined largely independently of the attacked input. We introduce Strike, an input-conditioned black-box adversarial robustness-testing framework that constructs and searches a hierarchical perturbation space for each input. Strike first partitions code into blocks and uses an LLM to generate context-specific structural candidates, filtered for syntactic validity, ranked by similarity, and adaptively combined. It then refines the best structural variant through similarity-guided identifier substitutions drawn from dynamically constructed candidate pools. Evaluations across representative code intelligence tasks, including clone detection, vulnerability detection, and code summarization, demonstrate that Strike achieves higher attack success rates with competitive target- model query overhead. Strategy-specific static checks across all three tasks and execution-based validation on the executable Juliet vulnerability-detection subset provide complementary, task-bounded evidence regarding the validity of the generated variants. Representation similarity and human evaluation further indicate that the variants remain highly similar to the original code and contextually natural. Fine-tuning with Strike- generated samples improves cross-attack performance on fixed perturbed evaluation sets while preserving clean performance.

</details>


### [349] [Refusal without Discrimination: What Encoded Prompts Do to Safety-Trained Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26176)
*Haoyu Zhang,Haowen Xu,Xiao Luo,Mohammad Zandsalimy,Shanu Sushmita*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 编码提示攻击的评测几乎只报告"有害臂"（模型对混淆有害请求的服从率），但本文证明该指标几乎不携带关于被测模型的信息：四个独立后训练的7-8B模型在有害同形字编码提示上的拒绝率跨度仅0.08（落在n=100采样噪声0.10的上限内），而同样四个模型对相同请求的明文版本拒绝率跨度达0.57。编码破坏的不是拒绝能力，而是判别力——某模型的有害/良性拒绝率差从明文+0.82降到编码下恰好0.00（两者均被拒绝0.99）。仅看有害臂的基准会给判别力迥异的两个模型打相同分数。进一步用同一基座权重跑完整SFT→DPO→RLVR流程发现后训练无法修复该损失：明文危害判别力从+0.55升到+0.80，但编码导致的损失不变（0.34–0.50），且在所有测试编码上标准有害臂指标与判别力的移动方向相反。作者报告了八个仪器缺陷及其对应的对照实验，这些缺陷在行为轴上均系统性抬高表观安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前编码提示（encoded-prompt）攻击评测几乎完全依赖"有害臂"这一单一指标：向模型发送混淆后的有害请求，报告其服从/拒绝率，并以此作为模型安全性的度量。作者质疑这一做法是否真的能区分模型，因为该指标缺乏良性对照，无法区分"模型有判别力地拒绝有害内容"与"模型对任何输入都一律拒绝"这两种截然不同的行为。若有害臂不携带关于被测模型的信息，那么大量基准结论、安全排名与后训练效果评估都可能建立在无信息量的测量之上。

Method: 1）选取四个独立后训练的7-8B模型，在同一批请求上分别测量明文与同形字（homoglyph）编码的有害臂拒绝率，并统计其跨模型跨度，与n=100采样噪声上限（0.10）对比；2）引入良性对照，计算有害与良性请求的拒绝率差（判别力Δ），观察编码前后该差值的变化；3）构造判别力相差极大的两个模型，检验仅看有害臂的基准是否给出一致评分；4）用已发表的训练配方在相同基座权重上跑SFT→DPO→RLVR完整流程，追踪明文判别力与编码诱导损失各自随训练阶段的演变；5）在多种编码上比较标准有害臂指标与判别力的移动方向；6）系统排查评测仪器缺陷，为每个缺陷配备能捕获它的对照实验。

Result: 四个模型在有害编码提示上的拒绝率跨度仅0.08，落在0.10的采样噪声上限之内；而相同请求明文条件下的跨度达0.57。某模型有害/良性拒绝率差由明文+0.82降至编码下恰好0.00（良性0.99、有害0.99），另一模型仍保留+0.61的差值，但仅读有害臂的基准将两者评分为相同。后训练流程中，明文危害判别力由+0.55提升到+0.80，而编码诱导的损失始终维持在0.34–0.50不变；在每一种测试编码上，标准有害臂指标的移动方向都与判别力相反。作者共报告八个仪器缺陷，全部在行为轴上系统性地抬高表观安全性，且只有借助非常规对照才能发现。

Conclusion: 编码提示攻击评测中的有害臂指标几乎不携带关于被测模型的信息，它测量的是无差别的拒绝倾向而非安全判别力，因此会同等评分判别力悬殊的模型并误判后训练效果。编码诱导的判别力损失无法被现有后训练流程（SFT→DPO→RLVR）修复，且标准有害臂指标与真实判别力的变化方向相反。作者报告的八个仪器缺陷方向一致地虚高表观安全性，表明该领域需要将良性对照与判别力度量纳入常规评测；缺少这些对照，上述所有问题均不可见。

Abstract: Encoded-prompt attacks are evaluated almost entirely on their harmful arm: a benchmark sends obfuscated harmful requests and reports how often the model complied. We show that this arm carries almost no information about the model under test. Across four independently post-trained 7-8B models, refusal of harmful homoglyph-encoded prompts spans 0.08 -- inside the 0.10 ceiling that sampling noise alone produces at n=100 -- while the same four models span 0.57 on the identical requests in plaintext. What the encoding destroys is not refusal but discrimination: on one model the gap between harmful and benign refusal falls from +0.82 in plaintext to exactly 0.00 under the encoding, benign and harmful requests being refused at an identical 0.99. A benchmark reading only the harmful arm scores that model and one retaining a +0.61 gap identically. We then ask whether post-training repairs this, using a published recipe on identical base weights. It does not: across a full SFT -> DPO -> RLVR pipeline, plaintext harm discrimination improves from +0.55 to +0.80 while the encoding-induced loss is unchanged at 0.34-0.50, and on every encoding tested the standard harmful-arm metric moves in the opposite direction to discrimination. None of this is visible without controls the field does not routinely run. We report eight instrument defects, each with the control that caught it; they share a direction, in that every defect on the behaviour axis inflated apparent safety.

</details>


### [350] [Design and Evaluation of a Controlled Post-Alert Incident Orchestration and Response Subsystem Using a Rule Engine and a Local Large Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26316)
*Hoang-Lam Huynh,Quoc-Cuong Tang,Van-Tri Phan,Khuong Nguyen-An*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于教育信息系统的受控告警后事件编排与响应子系统，通过分层架构实现确定性分类、上下文分析、人工审批与技术执行，并在实验室范围内验证了功能正确性、可追溯性与受控恢复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 教育信息系统的告警后处理需要兼顾自动化响应与安全可控，既要求分类与执行的确定性，又需要引入大语言模型等上下文分析能力而不失控。现有方案往往难以在自动化效率、可追溯性与模型集成的安全性之间取得平衡，因此需要一个分层、受控的编排与响应架构。

Method: 系统架构分离了确定性分类、上下文分析、人工审批和技术执行四个环节。由规则引擎判定严重程度并选择处置手册；静态RAG与本地大语言模型在验证器、护栏、输出净化器和安全回退等控制机制下提供建议内容。实验在模拟告警存入Elasticsearch后开始，并通过边界用例、持久队列、竞争实验和顺序测量等进行评估。

Result: 规则引擎在全部30个边界用例中与预定义路由矩阵完全一致。持久队列完成100个事件处理，未出现重复任务、新失败任务或非预期防火墙规则。八告警竞争实验保持了配置的单次活动模型请求上限，30次顺序测量显示告警后平均处理时间约为33秒。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在所评估的实验室范围内，该系统实现了功能正确性、可追溯性、受控恢复以及有界的模型集成，为教育信息系统的告警后编排与响应提供了一种可控方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a controlled post-alert incident orchestration and response subsystem for educational information systems. The architecture separates deterministic classification, contextual analysis, human approval, and technical execution. A Rule Engine determines severity and selects the playbook, while Static RAG and a local large language model provide advisory content under Validator, Guardrail, Output Sanitizer, and Safe Fallback controls. Experiments begin after simulated alerts are stored in Elasticsearch. The Rule Engine matched the predefined routing matrix in all 30 boundary cases. The Durable Queue completed 100 events without duplicate tasks, new failed tasks, or unintended firewall rules. An eight-alert contention experiment preserved the configured limit of one active model request, and 30 sequential measurements showed an overall mean post-alert processing time of approximately 33 seconds. The results demonstrate functional correctness, traceability, controlled recovery, and bounded model integration within the evaluated laboratory scope.

</details>


### [351] [Unread or Unenforced? Separating Representation from Enforcement Failure in Content Guards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26178)
*Haoyu Zhang,Yi Feng,Mohammad Zandsalimy,Shanu Sushmita*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种仅需一次前向传播、无需裁判模型的方法，通过读取内容守卫（content guard）自身的残差流探针与判决 logits，将"编码攻击绕过"拆分为"未表征有害内容"与"已表征但未拦截"两类相反性质的失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端攻击成功率把两种失败混为一个数字：一是表征能力上限（更多安全训练也无法解决），二是决策规则失当（安全训练可解决）。二者补救方向相反，因此必须区分。此前缺乏廉价的、可honest地做到这一点的测量手段。

Method: 将明文上拟合的内容探针不加重新拟合地迁移到编码条件，配合守卫的判决 logits 进行测量，代价仅为一次前向传播且无需裁判模型。为排除伪影，采用置换检验授权解码测量、长度匹配零假设，以及在基础模型可证明无法解码的条件上校准的对照下限；并用两个无共享输入的筛选器交叉验证拒绝条件的一致性。

Result: 置换检验最初在 19 个条件中许可 17 个（一个开放守卫）和 12 个（另一个）；加入长度匹配零假设与对照下限后均缩减至 4 个。被丢弃的并非边缘结果：最初最大的发现（守卫以 AUROC 0.72 表征某密码却零拦截）实为基础模型解码率为零的编码上的伪影。两个筛选器对拒绝条件完全一致。幸存的是真实但更窄的策略失败：在守卫能大量拦截的条件下，每 100 条提示有 7–23 条被表征却未拦截；在一个几乎不拦截的条件上为 56/100。"未解码却被拦截"几乎为零，说明两者均非仅对编码表象反应。对于真正的密码，两个守卫基本零拦截，这些单元被报告为未测量而非解码失败证据。

Conclusion: 区分表征失败与决策失败对守卫安全评估至关重要，朴素测量会系统性夸大问题。经严格校准后，真实的策略失败虽存在但范围明显更窄，且主要体现在守卫重拦截的条件下；对真密码，守卫近乎零拦截应视为未测量而非失败证据。

Abstract: When an encoded attack passes a content guard, the guard either never represented the payload's harmful content or represented it and failed to act. End-to-end attack success rate reports one number for both, yet the remedies are opposite: one is a representational limit that more safety training cannot reach, the other a decision rule that it can. We separate them by reading a guard's own residual stream -- a content probe fitted on plaintext and transferred, without refitting, to the encoded condition -- alongside its verdict logits, at the cost of one forward pass and no judge model.
  Doing this honestly is most of the problem, and it is our main contribution. A permutation test licenses the decode measurement on 17 of 19 conditions for one open guard and 12 of 19 for another; a length-matched null and a control floor calibrated on conditions the guard's base model provably cannot decode reduce both to 4. The discarded cells are not marginal ones: the largest result in our first analysis -- a guard representing a cipher at AUROC 0.72 while blocking none of it -- is an artefact on an encoding its base model decodes at rate zero. On one guard, two screens sharing no input agree exactly on which conditions to reject.
  What survives is a policy failure that is real but narrower than the uncontrolled analysis claimed: 7 to 23 per 100 prompts represented and not blocked on conditions the guards block heavily, and 56 per 100 on one condition a guard barely blocks. Blocked without decoding is near zero throughout, so neither guard reacts to the appearance of encoding rather than to content. On genuine ciphers both guards block essentially nothing, and we report those cells as unmeasured rather than as evidence of failure to decode.

</details>


### [352] [Dynamic Deep Prompt Optimization for Defending Against Jailbreak Attacks on LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26185)
*Doniyorkhon Obidov,Honggang Yu,Xiaolong Guo,Kaichen Yang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出DDPO，首个基于深度提示优化的越狱防御方法，利用目标LLM中间层动态生成防御嵌入并注入后续层，无需修改权重，在多种模型和攻击上显著优于静态提示优化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM虽能力强但仍易受越狱攻击，产生有害或非预期内容。模型微调虽可做安全对齐，但成本高且易发生灾难性遗忘。提示优化是替代方案，然而现有基于提示的防御多依赖静态修改（如固定前缀或后缀），无法适应多样且不断演变的攻击。

Method: DDPO利用目标LLM自身的中间层作为特征提取器，通过轻量级多层感知机（MLP）动态生成防御嵌入；随后将这些定制嵌入注入到后续的中间层，实现输入依赖的防御，同时不修改LLM权重，从而在最小计算开销下保证高适应性。

Result: 在多种模型和攻击上的实验表明，DDPO显著优于静态提示优化方法，尤其在弱对齐模型上，以及处理语义模糊的良性提示时表现突出，能成功将其与真正有害的请求区分开。

Conclusion: DDPO作为首个基于深度提示优化的越狱防御方法，通过动态、输入依赖的防御嵌入注入，在不改动模型权重的前提下兼顾适应性与低开销，为LLM安全防御提供了有效新思路。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) demonstrate impressive capabilities across many applications but remain vulnerable to jailbreak attacks, which elicit harmful or unintended content. While model fine-tuning is an option for safety alignment, it is costly and prone to catastrophic forgetting. Prompt optimization has emerged as a promising alternative, yet existing prompt-based defenses typically rely on static modifications (e.g., fixed prefixes or suffixes) that cannot adapt to diverse and evolving attacks.
  We propose Dynamic Deep Prompt Optimization (DDPO), the first jailbreak defense based on deep prompt optimization. DDPO uses the target LLM's own intermediate layers as feature extractors to dynamically generate defensive embeddings via a lightweight multilayer perceptron. These tailored embeddings are then injected into a subsequent intermediate layer, enabling an input-dependent defense without modifying the LLM's weights. This design ensures high adaptability with minimal computational overhead.
  Experiments on a diverse set of models and attacks demonstrate that DDPO significantly outperforms static prompt optimization methods, particularly on weakly aligned models and when handling semantically ambiguous benign prompts, successfully distinguishing them from genuinely harmful requests.

</details>


### [353] [Who Assures the Verifier? An Executable Assurance-Locus Audit of the European Digital Identity Wallet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26220)
*Anton Sokolov*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对欧盟数字身份钱包（EUDI Wallet）中依赖方（RP）验证环节的证据链进行"保证位点审计"，指出钱包侧认证日趋完善，但缺乏可独立复跑的证据证明具体验证器版本确实执行了请求、呈现与依赖决策控制。作者设计17条规则研究画像、机器可读证据收据与36个冻结合成交易，并由三种实现（Python、JavaScript、jq）执行108个案例，实现零预言差异与零跨路径分歧，进而提出"RP作为受测系统"的证据单元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EUDI Wallet 架构将实质义务置于依赖方：注册服务与用途、向 Wallet Unit 认证、验证呈现与信任锚、并做基于风险的状态决策。钱包认证与新兴的功能一致性评估框架（FCAF）提供了日益结构化的钱包侧证据，但一个不同的问题仍悬而未决：有何可独立复跑的证据能表明某笔交易中实际使用的具体 RP 验证器版本确实执行了适用的请求、呈现与依赖决策控制？现有公共代码库虽含大量协议验证机制，却缺少单一经审计的证据对象把 RP 注册与用途、确切的验证器/策略版本、交易裁决与下游属性使用连接起来。因此本文从"保证落点"角度审计现行法律、ARF 3.0.0、ETSI 元数据、FCAF 范围与三个固定版本开源验证器代码库，填补这一证据缺口。

Method: 1) 对现行法律、ARF 3.0.0、ETSI 元数据、FCAF 范围及三个 pinned 开源验证器代码库进行"保证位点审计"；2) 设计包含17条规则的研究画像、机器可读的证据收据（evidence receipt）以及36个冻结的合成交易；3) 由三种异质的研究自建实现（Python、JavaScript、jq）执行108个案例；4) 规划对26位受任命专家的预注册普查，以检验内容效度与治理可行性（招募待适用伦理/数据保护裁定）。

Result: 三种实现执行108个案例，实现零预言（oracle）不匹配与零跨路径分歧，证明了所提决策模型的确定性与可实现性（而非产品一致性或认证）。源码审查发现三个公共代码库均含实质性协议验证机制，但不存在单一经审计的证据对象，能同时联结 RP 注册与用途、确切的验证器/策略版本、交易裁决及下游属性使用。

Conclusion: 作者据此提出"RP作为受测系统"（RP-as-system-under-test）的证据单元，作为对钱包认证、注册与监管的补充而非替代。实验结果仅确立决策模型的确定性与可实现性，不代表产品合规或认证；26位专家的预注册普查将用于检验内容效度与治理可行性，招募尚待伦理与数据保护方面的裁定。

Abstract: The European Digital Identity Wallet (EUDI Wallet) architecture places material duties on relying parties: they register services and intended uses, authenticate to Wallet Units, validate presentations and trust anchors, and make risk-based status decisions. Wallet certification and the emerging Functional Conformance Assessment Framework provide increasingly structured wallet-side evidence. A different question remains: what independently rerunnable evidence shows that the concrete relying-party verifier version used in a transaction enforced the applicable request, presentation and reliance-decision controls? We conduct an assurance-locus audit of current law, Architecture and Reference Framework (ARF) 3.0.0, ETSI metadata, FCAF scope and three pinned open-source verifier codebases. We then design a 17-rule research profile, a machine-readable evidence receipt and 36 frozen synthetic transactions. Three heterogeneous study-authored implementations (Python, JavaScript and jq) execute 108 cases with zero oracle mismatches and zero cross-path disagreements. The experiment establishes determinism and implementability of the proposed decision model, not product conformance or certification. Source inspection finds substantial protocol-verification mechanisms in all three public codebases but no single audited evidence object joining RP registration and purpose, exact verifier/policy version, transaction verdict and downstream attribute use. We therefore propose an RP-as-system-under-test evidence unit that complements, rather than displaces, wallet certification, registration and supervision. A preregistered census of 26 appointed experts is prepared to test content validity and governance feasibility; recruitment awaits the applicable ethics/data-protection determination.

</details>


### [354] [Quantum-Ready Secure WAN: A Risk Assessment and Migration Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26225)
*Saeed Alam*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向企业广域网（WAN）的厂商中立后量子迁移框架，将暴露优先级、迁移就绪度与证据置信度分离，并映射到分阶段的安全、运营与治理关卡；同时给出TLS 1.3与IKEv2/IPsec中经典与ML-KEM混合密钥建立的完整评估设计。保留的工件范围较窄，仅含作者报告的TLS时延聚合数据、算术校验器与有限环境说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业WAN使用易受量子攻击的公钥密码来完成对等认证与密钥建立（IPsec、TLS、SD-WAN服务）。"先收集、后解密"已对保护周期较长的数据构成威胁，未来密码学相关量子计算机还将危及证书等认证依赖。后量子标准与政府指令虽已定义算法与过渡里程碑，但未告诉企业应优先迁移哪些WAN服务，也未说明何种证据足以支撑部署。因此需要一套决策与评估框架。

Method: 提出厂商中立框架，将暴露优先级、迁移就绪度、证据置信度三类输出分离，并映射到分阶段的安全、运营与治理关卡；设计针对TLS 1.3与IKEv2/IPsec中经典与ML-KEM混合密钥建立的完整评估方案。保留工件包含：作者报告的聚合TLS p50/p95/p99时延（配置丢包率0%、1%、3%、5%）、一个算术校验器，以及有限的环境与MTU/MSS说明。协议尺寸与MTU/MSS计算与实测行为分开报告。

Result: 在所述配置下，TLS-H与TLS-C的p95差值分别为0.00、0.16、0.38、0.63 ms，相对TLS-C为0.00%、1.32%、3.07%、5.00%。这些为描述性聚合观测值，而非推断性结果，因为每轮试验数据、样本数、原始抓包与带时间戳日志均未保留。不支持的IKE、资源、吞吐、重传与降级结论被排除。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了企业WAN后量子迁移的决策框架与评估设计，但保留工件仅支持有限的描述性时延观测；由于缺少逐轮数据与原始日志，结果不具备推断效力，相关协议尺寸分析与实测行为需分别看待，未支持的结论不应被采用。

Abstract: Enterprise wide-area networks (WANs) use quantum-vulnerable public-key cryptography to authenticate peers and establish keys for Internet Protocol Security (IPsec), Transport Layer Security (TLS), and software-defined WAN services. Harvest-now, decrypt-later collection already threatens data whose protection lifetime may exceed the time needed to migrate, while a future cryptographically relevant quantum computer would also endanger certificates and other authentication dependencies. Post-quantum standards and government directives now define algorithms and transition milestones, but they do not tell an enterprise which WAN services to migrate first or what evidence is sufficient for deployment. This paper proposes a vendor-neutral framework that separates exposure priority, migration readiness, and evidence confidence, and maps those outputs to staged security, operational, and governance gates. It also defines a complete evaluation design for classical and ML-KEM hybrid key establishment in TLS 1.3 and IKEv2/IPsec. The retained artifact is narrower: it contains author-reported aggregate TLS p50, p95, and p99 latencies at configured loss settings of 0%, 1%, 3%, and 5%, an arithmetic checker, and limited environment and MTU/MSS notes. At those settings, the TLS-H minus TLS-C p95 differences are 0.00, 0.16, 0.38, and 0.63 ms, corresponding to 0.00%, 1.32%, 3.07%, and 5.00% relative to TLS-C. These values are descriptive aggregate observations, not inferential results, because per-trial data, sample counts, original captures, and timestamped logs were not retained. Protocol-size and MTU/MSS calculations are reported separately from measured behavior, and unsupported IKE, resource, throughput, retransmission, and downgrade outcomes are excluded.

</details>


### [355] [eBPF Security in the Wild: Structural Concentration, Failure Mechanisms, and Discovery Gaps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26254)
*Baihong Chen,Hua Ming,Weifeng Pan,Tian Xie,Xiaojun Qi,Wen Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对eBPF漏洞开展实证研究，构建多源数据集并用统一框架分析其结构集中性、机制失效模式、架构分布与测试发现的语义盲区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: eBPF是安全关键的内核执行框架，但其漏洞态势分散于不同组件、语义缺口和测试技术之中，缺乏统一认识。

Method: 整合Linux内核修复提交、syzbot报告和公开CVE/NVD记录构建多源数据集，按结构集中度、机制级失效模式、架构分布和代表性技术发现缺口进行统一分析；并开展基于评分标准的代表性技术比较及Linux v5.10上版本对齐的Syzkaller案例研究。

Result: 漏洞态势呈结构性集中而非广泛分散，主要集中于运行时执行、并发、对象生命周期管理和跨可信阶段的语义不一致等少数反复出现的系统级失效；分布不均，Runtime是主要暴露面，Verifier和JIT频率较低但安全边界特性独特；案例研究显示有效探索语义狭窄，发现集中在少量Runtime失效上。

Conclusion: 原始覆盖率不足以完整反映漏洞发现的有效性，需关注语义层面的探索深度。

Abstract: Extended Berkeley Packet Filter (eBPF) is a security-critical in-kernel execution framework, yet its vulnerability landscape remains fragmented across components, semantic gaps, and testing techniques. We present an empirical study of observed eBPF vulnerabilities. We construct a multi-source dataset from Linux kernel fixing commits, syzbot reports, and public CVE/NVD records, and analyze it through a unified framework covering structural concentration, mechanism-level failure modes, architectural distribution, and discovery gaps in representative techniques. Our results show that the observed eBPF vulnerability landscape is structurally concentrated rather than broadly dispersed across many unrelated weakness types. The dominant portion is associated with a limited set of recurring system-level failures, especially in runtime execution, concurrency, object lifecycle management, and semantic inconsistencies across trusted stages. These failures are unevenly distributed across the eBPF pipeline: Runtime is the dominant exposure surface, whereas the Verifier and JIT are lowerfrequency but structurally distinct security boundaries. A rubric-based comparison of representative techniques and a version-aligned Syzkaller case study on Linux v5.10 show that, despite visible raw coverage of Runtime, Verifier, and JIT, effective exploration is semantically narrow, and observed discoveries concentrate in a small subset of Runtime failures. Overall, raw coverage alone provides an incomplete view of discovery effectiveness.

</details>


### [356] [CRT-Decomposed $Σ$-Protocols for CSIDH](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26258)
*I. Dey,I. Cherkaoui*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种利用理想类群CRT结构的CSIDH群作用零知识知识证明，具备完美完备性、完美特殊诚实验证者零知识性和2-特殊可靠性，可经Unruh变换得到QROM下直线提取的非交互证明；实际安全性受限于未来参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CSIDH零知识证明方案在提取器中使用回绕、格约简或启发式采样，导致存在乘法分叉引理损失，且难以在QROM下实现直线提取。作者希望借助CSIDH-512已知精确群结构所蕴含的CRT分解，构造结构更简洁、提取更直接的证明系统。

Method: 利用群结构已知时理想类群的CRT分解构造CSIDH群作用的知识证明；证明完美完备性、完美特殊诚实验证者零知识性和2-特殊可靠性，其中秘密可由两个接受转录经每CRT分量一次减法和一次模逆恢复，提取器无回绕、无格约简、无启发式采样；每轮恰好两个响应，故用Unruh变换获得QROM下直线提取的非交互证明；在CSIDH-512类群上实例化，用机器在精确258比特模数上验证代数层，并进行指数模型下的协议级模拟。

Result: 代数层在10^4个随机实例上全部通过；蒙特卡洛可靠性速率在每个测试t上与证明的2^{-t}界在95%置信度内吻合；序列化签名大小与公式相差1.6%以内；给出插桩的群作用计数；缩放中间相遇攻击的实测代价符合预测的sqrt(q)规律。另证明两个边界结果：CRT分解不能扩大每轮挑战空间；公开CRT跳跃曲线会将经典密钥恢复代价从约2^{128.6}降至约2^{67.3}次群作用。

Conclusion: 该构造在结构上完整且当下正确，但仅在类数含大素因子的未来参数上才具备定量安全性。与CSI-FiSh、CSI-Otter和Tanuki进行了比较；基于该知识证明的紧安全盲签名留待未来工作。

Abstract: We construct a zero-knowledge proof of knowledge for the CSIDH group action that exploits the Chinese Remainder Theorem (CRT) structure of the ideal class group, available whenever the group structure is known exactly, as for CSIDH-512. We prove perfect completeness, perfect special honest-verifier zero-knowledge, and 2-special soundness, in which the secret is recovered from two accepting transcripts by one subtraction and one modular inversion per CRT component; no rewinding loop, lattice reduction, or heuristic sampling appears in the extractor. Because each round admits exactly two responses, Unruh's transform yields a non-interactive proof with straight-line extraction in the quantum random oracle model (QROM), removing the multiplicative forking-lemma loss. We instantiate the scheme on the CSIDH-512 class group, verify the algebraic layer by machine over the exact 258-bit modulus ($10^4$ random instances, all passing), and report protocol-level simulations in the exponent model: Monte Carlo soundness rates matching the proven $2^{-t}$ bound within 95\% confidence at every tested $t$, serialized signature sizes within 1.6\% of the formulas, instrumented action counts, and a scaled meet-in-the-middle attack whose measured cost follows the predicted $\sqrt{q}$ law. We further prove two delimiting results: CRT decomposition cannot enlarge the per-round challenge space, and publishing the CRT hop curves lowers classical key-recovery cost from about $2^{128.6}$ to about $2^{67.3}$ group action evaluations. The construction is therefore correct and structurally complete today, but quantitatively secure only on future parameters whose class number has large prime factors. We compare against CSI-FiSh, CSI-Otter, and Tanuki; a tightly secure blind signature from this proof of knowledge is left to future work.

</details>


### [357] [Image-Based Techniques and Ensemble Soft Voting for Malware Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26281)
*Sushant Rakesh Lokhande,Fabio Di Troia,Martin Jurecek,Mark Stamp*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出基于集成学习与软投票策略的恶意软件家族图像分类框架，融合手工特征、预训练网络嵌入与自训练CNN三类特征，在17个家族上取得80.2%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恶意软件家族分类对安全防护具有重要意义，而将二进制文件转为图像是常见做法；但单一特征表示与单一模型的能力有限，且不同转换策略下的表现差异较大，因此需要探索多特征、多模型的互补融合。

Method: 采用8种图像转换策略；三条特征提取路径：(1) HOG+Haralick纹理+38个统计特征的手工描述子；(2) VGG16、ResNet50、ViT-B/16三个预训练网络作为冻结特征提取器（去除分类头）的稠密嵌入；(3) 自训练CNN的512维嵌入。每条路径在所有8种转换类型上用机器学习分类器评估，并进一步构建软投票集成池（15个投票者，基于专用验证集筛选）。

Result: 单模型最佳结果：手工特征77.8%，预训练网络73.8%，自训练CNN 74.8%。最佳软投票集成达到80.2%，比最佳单模型提升2.4个百分点且具统计显著性；多样性定量分析证实不同特征表示具有互补性，其中手工描述子贡献最大。

Conclusion: 多特征表示与软投票集成能够有效提升恶意软件家族图像分类性能，特征间的互补性是性能增益的关键来源。

Abstract: In this chapter, we investigate image-based malware family classification using an ensemble learning framework and a soft voting strategy. We consider malware binaries that have been converted into images using eight distinct conversion strategies. Three complementary feature extraction tracks are applied to these images: handcrafted descriptors combining Histogram of Oriented Gradients (HOG) and Haralick texture features along with 38 statistical features; dense embeddings obtained from three pretrained neural networks (VGG16, ResNet50, and ViT-B/16), where each pretrained model is used as a frozen feature extractor with its classification head removed; and 512-dimensional embeddings derived from a custom Convolutional Neural Network (CNN) trained directly on the malware images. Each of the three feature extraction techniques is evaluated with machine learning classifiers across all eight image conversion types. The best individual results are 77.8% accuracy for the handcrafted features, 73.8% for the pretrained neural network track, and 74.8% for the custom CNN track. Then we consider various soft voting ensemble strategies, and we find that the best-performing soft voting pool--consisting of fifteen voters selected on a dedicated validation split--achieves 80.2% accuracy across the 17 malware families under consideration, a statistically significant improvement of 2.4 percentage points over the best individual model. A quantitative diversity analysis confirms that the different feature representations are complementary, with the handcrafted descriptors being the strongest contributors.

</details>


### [358] [Neural Fingerprints for Malware Analysis: An Image-Based Metric Learning Approach with Application to Cross-Domain Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26282)
*Manasa Deshagouni,Sayma Akther,Martin Jurecek,Mark Stamp*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出基于图像的度量学习方法，从恶意软件图像中提取神经网络指纹（定长嵌入），通过嵌入空间的最近邻搜索判定家族归属，实现零样本家族识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统恶意软件家族分类器在新家族出现时必须重新训练。作者希望构建一种无需重训练即可识别未知家族的方案，因此转向度量学习以生成相似性度量而非固定类别边界。

Method: 在 MalNet-Images-Tiny 与 MalImg 合并数据集（453 家族、96,769 张图像）上训练编码器；采用轻量级 CNN 结合 multi-proxy anchor loss 学习定长嵌入；使用最近邻检索进行家族判定，并用 FAISS 支持大规模图库检索。

Result: 在无家族重叠的 17 家族灰度数据集上零样本评估，取得 73.1% retrieval@1 与 90.5% 开集 AUROC；在同域设置下与两类传统范式竞争性相当；嵌入可跨数据集迁移；给出 retrieval@k、聚类纯度、轮廓系数、分离比、少样本准确率、开集检测及图像扰动鲁棒性等全面评估。

Conclusion: 嵌入式度量学习无需重训练即可识别未见家族，且提供可解释的相似度分数，并可通过 FAISS 扩展至大规模图库，是一种兼具零样本能力与可扩展性的恶意软件家族识别方案。

Abstract: Identifying the family of a newly observed malware sample is a core task in threat intelligence, yet conventional classifiers must be retrained whenever a new family appears. This chapter develops an image-based metric learning approach that instead learns to extract discriminative neural fingerprints--fixed-length embeddings--from malware-as-image representations, so that family membership can be determined by nearest-neighbor search in the embedding space. The central advantage of this formulation is zero-shot capability: because the learned embedding induces a similarity metric rather than a fixed set of class boundaries, families that were never seen during training can be recognized by comparison against a gallery, with no retraining. We demonstrate this directly by training an encoder on MalNet-Images-Tiny and MalImg combined (453 families, 96,769 images) and evaluate it zero-shot on a held-out 17-family grayscale dataset with no family overlap. Using a lightweight CNN with multi-proxy anchor loss, this model attains 73.1% retrieval@1 and 90.5% open-set AUROC on families the encoder has never seen. We benchmark our embedding approach against two conventional paradigms in a same-domain setting, where all three are competitive at classifying malware into families. We further show that the learned embeddings transfer across datasets. Unlike classifiers, our embedding approach also yields interpretable similarity scores and scales to large galleries via Facebook AI Similarity Search (FAISS). Finally, we provide a comprehensive evaluation of the learned embedding space using retrieval@k, cluster purity, silhouette score, separation ratio, few-shot accuracy, and open-set detection metrics, along with robustness analysis under image perturbations.

</details>


### [359] [A Throughput-Oriented Analytical Model for Post-Quantum Security Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26284)
*Ignazio Pedone,Stefano Pirandola*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向吞吐量的分析模型，为TLS和SSH中后量子安全连接建立的最大可持续速率提供紧致上界，并结合端点与网络约束识别瓶颈，实验验证其准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子威胁意识增强，推动业界在现有系统中集成抗量子密码，尤其后量子密码（PQC）会对网络端点性能和传输开销产生影响，进而影响TLS、SSH等安全协议的可扩展性。因此需要准确的网络仿真工具来评估PQC集成的实际效果。

Method: 提出面向吞吐量的分析模型，将握手过程分解为主导性密码运算时间和网络传输时间，同时考虑端点与网络容量约束，识别瓶颈并推导以每秒握手次数表示的可达吞吐量。实验采用NIST FIPS 203、204、205标准原语（含ML-KEM、ML-DSA），在实验测试床上验证模型精度。

Result: 实验结果与测试床数据对比表明模型具有较高准确性。将模型集成到网络环境中后，可支持多端点间的高效资源分配，从而在多单播场景中优化PQC流量。

Conclusion: 该分析模型能够为后量子安全连接建立速率提供紧致上界，兼具端点与网络约束的考量，可用于指导PQC在实际网络中的部署与资源优化。

Abstract: Growing awareness of the impact of quantum threat on classical cryptography directly translates into a growing demand for accurate network simulation tools capable of estimating the integration effects of quantum-safe cryptography in current systems. In particular, the adoption of Post-Quantum Cryptography (PQC) has a direct impact on the performance of network endpoints and transmission overhead. This also affects the scalability of widely adopted security protocols such as TLS and SSH. In this paper, we present a throughput-oriented analytical model that provides a tight upper bound on the maximum sustainable rate of post-quantum secure connection establishment in TLS and SSH. This model takes into account both endpoint and network capacity constraints, decomposing the handshake process into dominant cryptographic operation time and network transmission time. Identifying the bottleneck allows us to derive the achievable throughput in terms of handshakes per second. The experimental results provided show the accuracy of the model against the data obtained from an experimental testbed using, among others, NIST standard primitives from FIPS 203, 204, and 205, including ML-KEM and ML-DSA. Finally, we integrate our model into a network environment and demonstrate how it can be leveraged to enable efficient resource allocation among multiple endpoints, optimizing PQC traffic in multiple-unicast scenarios.

</details>


### [360] [On the security and privacy of LLMs in Mobility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26295)
*Mauro Conti,Lorenzo Perinello,Umberto Salviati*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文综述生成式AI（尤其是LLM）在交通出行领域的应用，并按欧盟AI法案要求提炼9类技术指标评估现有研究，发现研究集中在GPT/Llama模型与交通应用，普遍忽视安全、隐私与可靠性，且合规性严重不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 出行行业正因生成式AI的进步发生范式转变，仅汽车市场年规模约2.9万亿美元，涉及超15亿辆车。LLM在出行领域加速采用，带来网络安全、隐私与可靠性等新挑战。同时欧盟AI法案将交通AI列为高风险，亟需评估现有研究是否满足其要求。

Method: 本文开展综述研究，从欧盟AI法案的高风险要求中提炼出9个技术类别，用以评估当前研究与未来部署。共审查35项相关工作，重点分析所采用的模型类型、应用场景以及在安全、隐私、可靠性和合规方面的覆盖情况。

Result: 研究发现，超过50%的被审查工作主要研究GPT和Llama模型及交通类应用，而安全、隐私和可靠性被大幅忽视。在35项被审查工作中，仅1项包含部分漏洞评估，仅1项涉及部分风险管理系统。

Conclusion: 研究揭示出强大优化性能与法规遵从之间存在明显鸿沟，合规受限更多源于过度关注静态性能而非技术本身，并强调在安全关键型智能交通系统中亟需采用“安全设计”理念。

Abstract: The mobility sector is undergoing a paradigm shift driven by advances in Generative Artificial Intelligence. With a global market valued at approximately 2.9 trillion dollars annually, considering only cars, the integration of these technologies has the potential to impact more than 1.5 billion vehicles worldwide. As Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly adopted in mobility, concerns about cybersecurity, privacy, and reliability emerge. Accordingly, this paper surveys current applications and assesses these challenges. Since the European AI Act classifies transportation AI as high risk, we derive nine technical classes from its requirements to assess current research and future deployments. Our findings show that research mainly studies GPT and Llama models (over 50\% of reviewed works) and traffic applications while largely neglecting security, privacy, and reliability. This gap extends to AI Act compliance: among 35 reviewed works, only one includes a partial vulnerability assessment and one a partial risk management system. We identify a clear gap between strong optimization performance and regulatory adherence, suggesting compliance is limited less by technology than by a focus on static performance over lifecycle safety, and underscoring an urgent need for security-by-design in safety-critical intelligent transportation systems.

</details>


### [361] [Staged Multi-step UTXO Workflows via Recursive Invariants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26305)
*Shuyang Tang,Sherman S. M. Chow,Hongfei Fu,Zihan Guo,Guoqiang Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出递归不变量（RIs）及其DSL与工具链，用于在无状态UTXO执行模型中表达多步工作流规则，实现验证时本地性与显式成本核算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无状态UTXO风格执行通过本地与引用数据验证交易，支持并行验证和可预测的序列化大小/权重核算。但多步工作流需在输出间传递状态，若另一个有效花费先确认，已准备的下一步交易可能失效。显式状态传递将一致性维护、链下跟踪与交易重建推到协议边界，增加协调成本与延迟。

Method: 提出递归不变量（RIs）作为交易级逻辑与工具链，用交易级谓词表达工作流规则，基于交易输入与RI引用的索引后继位置。当实现某后继位置的交易被接受时，一步后重新检查前驱的RI，将规则向前传递，无需共享可变应用状态或可执行输出逻辑。针对部分后继子句在验证时不可检查的问题，设计小型静态类型DSL，采用三值语义（真、假、未知）延迟未来依赖义务。框架形式化UTXO验证与账本扩展，识别验证时可求值的一步片段，并证明演绎系统相对三值语义的可靠性。给出该模型的验证与账本扩展算法。实现原型RI解释器与基准工具链，覆盖六个工作负载。

Result: 六个实践驱动的案例研究显示累计验证成本代理近似线性增长，并展示无需预先构造每个后继的分阶段工作流约束。原型RI解释器与基准工具链覆盖六个工作负载。

Conclusion: RIs使多步协议规则保持验证时本地性并支持显式成本核算，跨交易保证由重复的一步检查产生，为无状态UTXO中多步工作流提供可行方案。

Abstract: Stateless UTXO-style execution validates transactions using local and referenced data, enabling parallel validation and predictable serialized-size/weight accounting. Multi-step workflows thread state across outputs, and a prepared next-step transaction may become stale if another valid spend confirms first. Explicit state threading therefore shifts consistency maintenance, off-chain tracking, and transaction rebuilding to the protocol boundary, increasing coordination cost and latency. Recursive invariants (RIs), our proposed transaction-level logic and toolchain, address this gap by expressing workflow rules as transaction-level predicates over a transaction's inputs and indexed successor positions referenced by the RI. An accepted transaction realizing such a successor position re-checks the predecessor's RI one step later, carrying the workflow rule forward without shared mutable application state or executable output logic. Thus, multi-step protocol rules preserve validation-time locality and admit explicit cost accounting, while cross-transaction guarantees arise from repeated one-step checking. Not all successor clauses are checkable at validation time, so our small statically typed domain-specific language (DSL) uses three-valued semantics (true, false, unknown) to defer future-dependent obligations until checkable. Co-designed with this DSL, our framework formalizes UTXO validation and ledger extension, identifies the validation-time-evaluable one-step fragment, and proves the deduction system sound w.r.t. the three-valued semantics. We give validation and ledger-extension algorithms for this model. We implement a prototype RI interpreter and benchmarking toolchain for six workloads. Six practice-motivated case studies exhibit roughly linear cumulative validation-cost proxy growth and illustrate staged workflow constraints without preconstructing each successor.

</details>


### [362] [HYDRA: Proactive Android Malware Drift Adaptation via Hierarchical Graph Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26352)
*Han Chen,Hanchen Wang,Hongmei Chen,Lu Qin,Wenjie Zhang,Ying Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: HYDRA：一种主动式概念漂移适应框架，通过混合图结构与跨域对比学习，学习漂移不变表示，显著降低误报漏报并减少标注需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Android恶意软件快速演化导致概念漂移，严重削弱机器学习检测器性能。现有适应策略要么被动（性能下降后才响应且依赖大量人工标注），要么虽主动但依赖不稳定的对抗训练和单一层级、不完整的图表示。

Method: HYDRA首先以混合图结构建模应用，结合细粒度控制流图（CFG）与粗粒度函数调用图（FCG）以捕获全面行为模式；随后引入跨域对比学习目标，对齐历史（源）与新（目标）数据分布；通过为目标无标签样本生成伪标签，将语义相似应用在单一稳定优化过程中拉近，统一特征学习与域对齐，无需复杂对抗目标。

Result: 在大规模、按时间排序的恶意软件数据集上，HYDRA的假阴性率与假阳性率显著低于最先进基线，同时所需标注样本最多减少87.5%。

Conclusion: HYDRA为安全应用中的概念漂移问题提供了一种鲁棒且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Concept drift, driven by the rapid evolution of Android malware, severely degrades the performance of machine learning detectors. Current adaptation strategies are often reactive, responding only after performance has dropped and imposing a significant manual annotation burden, or they are proactive but rely on unstable adversarial training and incomplete, single-level graph representations. To overcome these limitations, we propose HYDRA (Hybrid Drift Adaptation), a proactive adaptation framework that learns drift-invariant representations from hierarchically structured data. HYDRA first models applications using a hybrid graph structure, combining fine-grained Control Flow Graphs (CFGs) and coarse-grained Function Call Graphs (FCGs) to capture comprehensive behavioral patterns. It then introduces a novel cross-domain contrastive learning objective that aligns historical (source) and new (target) data distributions. By generating pseudo-labels for unlabeled target samples, our method pulls representations of semantically similar applications together, regardless of their domain, within a single, stable optimization process. This approach unifies feature learning and domain alignment, eliminating the need for complex adversarial objectives. Extensive experiments on large-scale, time-ordered malware datasets demonstrate that HYDRA achieves substantially lower False Negative and False Positive Rates than state-of-the-art baselines while requiring up to 87.5% fewer labeled samples. Our work thus offers a robust and efficient solution to combat concept drift in security applications.

</details>


### [363] [Formally Modeling the Terrapin Attack on SSH](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26358)
*Jörg Schwenk,Fabian Bäumer,Marcus Brinkmann*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个"部分选择状态下的信道完整性"形式化模型，用于分析 SSH 中不同 AEAD 模式在 Terrapin 攻击下及更强攻击模型下的安全性，并给出具体安全界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Terrapin 攻击（USENIX Security 2024）揭示了"信道状态攻击"这一新向量，但不同 AEAD 模式受影响程度不同，"未受影响"是否等于"安全"仍是未解问题。现有安全信道形式化模型基于有状态加密，却未定义信道状态是什么、如何作为输入用于不同 AEAD 模式。

Method: 提出部分选择状态下的信道完整性形式化模型，以 SSH 序列号作为被选择状态。使用抽象的有状态加密接口，为 SSH 中八种主流 AEAD 模式提供伪代码描述。通过变化 SND 预言机，建模仅密文（CO，即 Terrapin 攻击）、已知明文（KPA）与选择明文（CPA）三类攻击。

Result: 三种 Encrypt-then-MAC（EtM）模式与 SSH 中的 ChaCha20-Poly1305 在 CO 模型下不安全；AES-GCM 是唯一在三种模型变体下均安全的密码。此外，CBC 加密的 Encrypt-and-MAC（EaM）即使在 KPA 模型下也安全，并给出一种新的类 BEAST 选择明文攻击，可分离该方案下的 KPA 与 CPA 模型。

Conclusion: 该模型为 SSH 信道完整性提供了统一的形式化分析框架，厘清了各 AEAD 模式的安全边界，并证明"未被 Terrapin 影响"并不等于"安全"。研究还通过新攻击区分了 EaM-CBC 的 KPA 与 CPA 安全性。

Abstract: The Terrapin attack against SSH channel integrity (USENIX Security 2024) used a novel attack vector: attacks on the channel state. Surprisingly, not all AEAD modes of SSH were equally affected by this attack, and it remained an open question if "unaffected" meant "secure". Existing formal models for secure channels are based on stateful encryption. However, these models do not define what the channel state is and how it is used as input to the different AEAD modes.
  In this paper, we propose a formal model for channel integrity under partially chosen state. Applied to the Terrapin attack, the chosen state is the SSH sequence number. It uses an abstract stateful encryption interface, for which we provide pseudocode descriptions for the eight most prominent AEAD modes used in SSH. By varying the SND oracle, we can model ciphertext-only (CO; the Terrapin attack), known-plaintext (KPA), and chosen-plaintext (CPA) attacks. This allows us to establish concrete bounds on the security of the AEAD modes. We find that all three Encrypt-then-MAC (EtM) modes and ChaCha20-Poly1305 in SSH are insecure in the CO model. AES-GCM is the only cipher secure in all three model variants. Going beyond Terrapin, we show that Encrypt-and-MAC (EaM) with a CBC cipher is secure, even in the KPA model. In particular, we describe a novel BEAST-like chosen-plaintext attack on the channel integrity of EaM-CBC, which separates the KPA and CPA models for this scheme.

</details>


### [364] [Rouxii: Exploiting Honeypots with Deception-Aware AI Pentesters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26555)
*Arthur Cordeiro,Alberto Maria Mongardini,Emmanouil Vasilomanolakis*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 研究提出Rouxii框架，让自主LLM渗透测试者主动识别并利用蜜罐，识别率从19%升至97%，并可将蜜罐反制其运营者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往评估多假设攻击者不知情蜜罐欺骗，缺少对"具备反欺骗能力的自主AI攻击者"这一对立情景的研究，因此需要考察欺骗有效性如何随攻击者知识变化。

Method: 提出AI驱动的渗透测试框架Rouxii，在侦察阶段整合反欺骗，从蜜罐检测转向利用；对比vanilla与anti-deception两种配置，覆盖3个推理模型、11种网络设置、12个周期，共1544份攻击报告，并做蜜罐白盒分析。

Result: 匹配队列中（仅提示不同）正确识别蜜罐从19%提升至97%，OT服务上从11%升至97%，对真实服务的误报维持在0.7%；PentestGPT、HackingBuddy等基线同样失败。白盒分析实现使Conpot停机而不触发存活监控的拒绝服务，以及污染GasPot上报情报。

Conclusion: 蜜罐欺骗效果高度依赖攻击者知识，被检测到的陷阱可被反向利用；评估蜜罐对AI攻击者的鲁棒性时必须考虑会主动推理并利用欺骗层的对手。

Abstract: Honeypots are designed to deceive attackers, and recent work shows they can also derail autonomous LLM-based pentesters. These evaluations, however, largely consider attackers unaware of the deception they face. We study the opposite setting: an autonomous attacker explicitly equipped to recognize and act on honeypot fingerprints. We introduce Rouxii, an AI-driven penetration-testing framework that integrates counter-deception into reconnaissance and pivots from honeypot detection to exploitation. We evaluate matched vanilla and anti-deception Rouxii configurations across three reasoning models and eleven network setups over twelve cycles (1,544 attack reports). Between the matched cohorts, which differ only in the prompt, counter-deception raises correct honeypot identification from 19% to 97%, an effect strongest on OT services (11% to 97%), while false alarms on the real service stay at 0.7%. Deception-unaware baselines (PentestGPT, HackingBuddy) fail similarly, indicating the effect is not specific to our framework. Detection, moreover, is not the endpoint: through a white-box analysis of the honeypots themselves we show that a detected trap can be turned against its operator, demonstrating a denial-of-service that disables Conpot without tripping its liveness monitoring, and a corruption of the intelligence a GasPot instance reports. These findings show that deception effectiveness depends strongly on attacker knowledge, and that evaluations of honeypot resilience against AI attackers must account for adversaries that actively reason about and exploit the deception layer.

</details>


### [365] [Decoding the Legalese: A Scalable and Quantitative Framework for Analyzing Corporate Privacy Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26680)
*Jiaming Tang,Chenlan Wang,Mingyan Liu,Armin Sarabi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文开发并评估了一个基于大语言模型（LLM）的端到端系统，将原始隐私政策转化为细粒度结构化表示和量化指标，构建了包含1万个网站隐私政策的数据集，并首次提出四个维度的标准化可重复评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐私政策是组织披露个人数据收集、处理和共享的主要机制，但因冗长和密集的法律语言难以被普通用户理解，且除监管要求外缺乏标准化指标来刻画隐私政策的关键质量特征。LLM的进展使大规模自动结构化和分析这些文档成为可能。

Method: 构建端到端、由LLM驱动的管道：应用详细分类体系提取具体数据要素与治理实践，并捕获将每项实践与其引用数据要素关联的关系链接；由此生成细粒度结构化表示和一组量化测度，并对1万份网站隐私政策语料库进行处理。

Result: 生成了迄今最全面的同类数据集（1万份网站隐私政策），并基于结构化表示提出首个标准化、可重复的隐私政策量化指标，覆盖完整性、透明度、对用户保护的承诺、对商业驱动数据实践的强调四个维度。

Conclusion: 该框架支持在行业内和跨行业比较隐私政策，并评估用户保护与商业利益之间的张力，为隐私政策的标准化度量提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Even though privacy policies are the primary mechanism organizations use to disclose how they collect, process, and share personal data, they are difficult for average users to interpret, perhaps by design, due to their verbosity and dense legal language. Importantly, there is a lack of standardized metrics that characterize key qualities of a privacy policy beyond regulatory requirements. Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) make it feasible to automatically structure and analyze these documents at scale. In this study, we develop and evaluate an end-to-end, LLM-enabled system that converts raw privacy policies into fine-grained structured representations and a set of quantitative measures. Our pipeline applies a detailed taxonomy to extract specific data elements and governing practices, capturing relational links that connect each practice to the data elements it references. We apply our framework to a diverse corpus of 10,000 website privacy policies, yielding, to the best of our knowledge, the most comprehensive dataset of its kind to date. Building on our structured representations, we introduce the first standardized and repeatable quantitative metrics for evaluating privacy policies along four dimensions: completeness, transparency, commitment to user protection, and emphasis on business-driven data practices. This allows us to compare policies within and across industry sectors, and to assess the tension between user protection and business interests.

</details>


### [366] [From Alignment to Access Control: A Framework for GenAI Policy Enforcement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26682)
*Nathalie Baracaldo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统梳理生成式AI应用中的策略执行现状，提出分析现有策略定义与执行方法的方法论，并给出建议与行动呼吁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI应用发展迅速，但安全与合规机制滞后，导致真实事件频发；同时"策略"在不同从业者中含义不一，造成混淆与割裂的方案，难以满足合规要求。

Method: 提出一套方法论，系统性地分析与拆解现实中已有的策略定义与执行方法，从"好的、坏的、丑的"三个维度进行审视与分类。

Result: 基于原则化分析，揭示了现有策略执行方案的优点、缺陷与严重问题，并识别出当前做法的局限。

Conclusion: 向社区提出建议并呼吁采取行动，以统一并改进GenAI应用的策略执行；本文为USENIX Security 2026 Enigma演讲的扩展姊妹篇。

Abstract: Generative AI (GenAI) applications have flourished enabling users to chat with large language models, and to create agents to act on their behalf for a variety of tasks. The pace of development of capabilities in this field is incredibly fast with security and safety taking a back seat. Unfortunately, the slower pace at which security and safety mechanisms have evolved has led to real incidents. Policy enables the definition of desirable behavior of applications, and for that reason, it is a cornerstone of making systems secure and compliant. Policy however means different things to different practitioners creating confusion and siloed solutions that are not adequate for compliance. This paper takes a tour of the good, the bad and the ugly when it comes to policy enforcement in GenAI applications. We propose a methodology to systematically analyze and dissect existing approaches to define and enforce policy found in the wild. Based on this principled analysis, we provide recommendations and call for action for the community to address.
  This paper is a companion extension of USENIX Security 2026 Enigma talk titled "From Alignment to Access Control: A Unified View of GenAI Policy Enforcement" by the author Nathalie Baracaldo.

</details>


### [367] [A2M: Trace-Optimized Agent Hijacking in the MCP Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26761)
*Laizhen Li,Xuan Wang,Peicheng Zhao,Juanjuan Zhao,Kejiang Ye,Cheng-zhong Xu,Xitong Gao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出A2M双阶段黑盒框架，通过优化MCP工具元数据与返回内容劫持智能体，攻击成功率高且具迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCP智能体依赖语义匹配从第三方服务器选择工具，攻击者可通过控制元数据和输出来利用这一供应链风险。

Method: A2M包含两阶段：Attraction阶段优化工具元数据提高调用概率；Manipulation阶段利用执行轨迹精炼对抗性工具返回来引导智能体。

Result: 在LiveMCPBench上，GLM-4.6直接攻击的恶意工具调用率达93.6%，加权token成本增至32.4倍，平均攻击成功率74.4%；迁移至其他四个模型分别为63.6%、2.7倍、24.5%。

Conclusion: 研究表明MCP生态需加强工具审查与运行时隔离，代码已开源。

Abstract: Agents using the Model Context Protocol (MCP) rely on semantic matching to select tools from third-party servers, exposing a semantic supply-chain risk through attacker-controlled metadata and outputs. We introduce A2M (Attraction-to-Manipulation), a two-stage black-box framework for hijacking MCP agents. The Attraction phase optimizes tool metadata to increase invocation probability; the Manipulation phase uses execution traces to refine adversarial tool returns that steer agents toward attacker-desired outcomes. On LiveMCPBench, direct attacks optimized and evaluated on GLM-4.6 achieve a macro-average malicious tool invocation rate of 93.6% across four scenarios, increase weighted token costs to 32.4$\times$ the benign baseline under Cognitive Denial of Service, and attain a mean attack success rate of 74.4% across Information Exfiltration, Environment Integrity Compromise, and Reasoning Derailment. Transfer to four other models without re-optimization yields corresponding macro-averages of 63.6%, 2.7$\times$, and 24.5%. These findings motivate stronger tool vetting and runtime isolation in MCP ecosystems. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/Lilaizhen/A2M.

</details>
